> DEVELOPER

I need you to audit the changes on the current branch vs main to determine if they're genuinely improving the codebase or busy work. This is a "desloppify" branch with ~50 commits and ~1200 changed files.

Please sample diffs from several categories of changes and assess each. Run these commands to sample:

1. `git diff main -- src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useSharedLightboxState.ts` - a hook that was supposedly refactored
2. `git diff main -- supabase/functions/claim-next-task/index.ts` - edge function changes
3. `git diff main -- src/shared/lib/preloading/service.ts` - preloading service changes
4. `git diff main -- src/shared/components/SegmentSettingsForm/SegmentSettingsForm.tsx` - component decomposition
5. `git diff main -- src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/utils/timeline-utils.ts` - utility changes
6. `git diff main -- src/shared/lib/media/downloadMedia.ts` - media utility changes
7. `git diff main -- src/shared/realtime/RealtimeConnection.ts` - realtime changes
8. `git diff main -- src/shared/components/ProductTour/index.tsx` - product tour changes
9. `git diff main -- src/integrations/supabase/bootstrap/createSupabaseClient.ts` - supabase client
10. `git diff main -- src/shared/lib/timelineWriteQueue.ts` - timeline write queue

Also check:
- `git diff main -- src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useSharedLightboxState.types.ts` (new file)
- `git diff main -- src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useSharedLightboxInteractionState.ts` (new file)
- `git diff main -- src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useSharedLightboxPresentationState.ts` (new file)

For each sample, assess:
- Is this a genuine improvement (bug fix, real decomposition, dead code removal, deduplication)?
- Or is it busy work (moving code around, renaming without benefit, splitting files unnecessarily, adding abstractions that don't help)?

Also run `git log main..HEAD --oneline | grep -i revert` to see how many reverts there are (reverts of previous work on this same branch suggest churn).

Be thorough and honest. Give a final verdict with a rough percentage of genuine improvements vs busy work.

-export function useLightboxNavigationModel(input: UseSharedLightboxStateInput): UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['navigation'] {

-    swipeDisabled,

-    hasPrevious,

-  });
-
-  return {
-    safeClose,
-    activateClickShield,
-    swipeNavigation,
-  };

    21→function useLightboxShotActions(
    22→  core: SharedLightboxCoreProps,
    23→  shots: SharedLightboxShotProps,
    24→): UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['shots'] {
    25→  const { media, selectedProjectId, onClose } = core;
    26→  const {
    27→    allShots,
    28→    onNavigateToShot,
    29→    onShotChange,
    30→    selectedShotId,
    31→    positionedInSelectedShot,
    32→    associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot,
    33→    optimisticPositionedIds,
    34→    optimisticUnpositionedIds,
    35→    onAddToShot,
    36→    onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
    37→    onShowTick,
    38→    onShowSecondaryTick,
    39→    onOptimisticPositioned,
    40→    onOptimisticUnpositioned,
    41→  } = shots;
    42→  const {
    43→    isCreatingShot,
    44→    quickCreateSuccess,
    45→    handleQuickCreateAndAdd,
    46→    handleQuickCreateSuccess,
    47→  } = useShotCreation({
    48→    media,
    49→    selectedProjectId,
    50→    allShots: allShots || [],
    51→    onNavigateToShot,
    52→    onClose,
    53→    onShotChange,
    54→  });
    55→  const computedPositionedInSelectedShot = useMemo(() => (
    56→    typeof positionedInSelectedShot === 'boolean' ? positionedInSelectedShot : undefined
    57→  ), [positionedInSelectedShot]);
    58→  const {
    59→    isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot,
    60→    isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
    61→    handleAddToShot,
    62→    handleAddToShotWithoutPosition,
    63→  } = useShotPositioning({
    64→    media,
    65→    selectedShotId,
    66→    allShots: allShots || [],
    67→    positionedInSelectedShot: computedPositionedInSelectedShot,
    68→    associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot,
    69→    optimisticPositionedIds,
    70→    optimisticUnpositionedIds,
    71→    onNavigateToShot,
    72→    onClose,
    73→    onAddToShot,
    74→    onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
    75→    onShowTick,
    76→    onShowSecondaryTick,
    77→    onOptimisticPositioned,
    78→    onOptimisticUnpositioned,
    79→  });
    80→
    81→  return {
    82→    isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot,
    83→    isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
    84→    handleAddToShot,
    85→    handleAddToShotWithoutPosition,
    86→    isCreatingShot,
    87→    quickCreateSuccess,
    88→    handleQuickCreateAndAdd,
    89→    handleQuickCreateSuccess,
    90→  };
    91→}
    92→
    93→function useSharedMakeMainVariantState(params: {
    94→  media: GenerationRow;
    95→  sourceGenerationData: GenerationRow | null;
    96→  isViewingNonPrimaryVariant: boolean;
    97→  activeVariant: GenerationVariant | null;
    98→  setPrimaryVariant: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
    99→  refetchVariants: () => void;
   100→  shotId?: string;
   101→  selectedShotId?: string;
   102→  onClose: () => void;
   103→}): UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['makeMainVariant'] {
   104→  const {
   105→    media,
   106→    sourceGenerationData,
   107→    isViewingNonPrimaryVariant,
   108→    activeVariant,
   109→    setPrimaryVariant,
   110→    refetchVariants,
   111→    shotId,
   112→    selectedShotId,
   113→    onClose,
   114→  } = params;
   115→  const canMakeMainVariantFromChild = !!sourceGenerationData && !!media.location;
   116→  const canMakeMainVariantFromVariant = isViewingNonPrimaryVariant && !!activeVariant?.location;
   117→  const canMakeMainVariant = canMakeMainVariantFromChild || canMakeMainVariantFromVariant;
   118→  const { isMakingMainVariant, handleMakeMainVariant } = useMakeMainVariant({
   119→    media,
   120→    sourceGenerationData,
   121→    canMakeMainVariantFromChild,
   122→    canMakeMainVariantFromVariant,
   123→    activeVariant,
   124→    setPrimaryVariant,
   125→    refetchVariants,
   126→    shotId,
   127→    selectedShotId,
   128→    onClose,
   129→  });
   130→
   131→  return {
   132→    canMake: canMakeMainVariant,
   133→    canMakeFromChild: canMakeMainVariantFromChild,
   134→    canMakeFromVariant: canMakeMainVariantFromVariant,
   135→    isMaking: isMakingMainVariant,
   136→    handle: handleMakeMainVariant,
   137→  };
   138→}

   140→export function useSharedLightboxInteractionState(
   141→  input: UseSharedLightboxStateInput,
   142→  variantsState: VariantsStateResult,
   143→): InteractionState {
   144→  const star = useStarToggle({
   145→    media: input.core.media,
   146→    starred: input.starred,

   152→    isVideo: input.core.isVideo,
   153→    selectedShotId: input.shots.selectedShotId,

   156→    media: input.core.media,

   158→    selectedProjectId: input.core.selectedProjectId,

   160→  const lineageState = useGenerationLineage({
   161→    media: input.core.media,
   162→    enabled: !input.core.isFormOnlyMode,
   163→  });
   164→  const shots = useLightboxShotActions(input.core, input.shots);
   165→  const { sourceGenerationData, sourcePrimaryVariant } = useSourceGeneration({
   166→    media: input.core.media,

   176→    shotId: input.shots.shotId,

   179→  });

1→import { useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
     2→import { useLightboxNavigation } from './useLightboxNavigation';
     3→import { useSwipeNavigation } from './useSwipeNavigation';
     4→import { useEffectiveMedia } from './useEffectiveMedia';
     5→import { useLayoutMode } from './useLayoutMode';
     6→import { invokeLightboxDelete } from '../utils/lightboxDelete';
     7→import type {
     8→  InteractionState,
     9→  LightboxButtonGroupProps,
    10→  SharedLightboxButtonGroupProps,
    11→  SharedLightboxCoreProps,
    12→  SharedLightboxNavigationProps,
    13→  UseSharedLightboxStateInput,
    14→  UseSharedLightboxStateReturn,
    15→  VariantsStateResult,
    16→} from './useSharedLightboxState.types';
    17→
    18→function useLightboxNavigationModel(
    19→  core: SharedLightboxCoreProps,
    20→  navigation: SharedLightboxNavigationProps,
    21→) {
    22→  const { onClose, readOnly } = core;
    23→  const {
    24→    hasNext = false,
    25→    hasPrevious = false,
    26→    handleSlotNavNext,
    27→    handleSlotNavPrev,
    28→    swipeDisabled,
    29→    showNavigation,
    30→  } = navigation;
    31→  const { safeClose, activateClickShield } = useLightboxNavigation({
    32→    onNext: handleSlotNavNext,
    33→    onPrevious: handleSlotNavPrev,
    34→    onClose,
    35→  });
    36→  const swipeNavigation = useSwipeNavigation({
    37→    onSwipeLeft: () => {
    38→      if (hasNext) handleSlotNavNext();
    39→    },
    40→    onSwipeRight: () => {
    41→      if (hasPrevious) handleSlotNavPrev();
    42→    },
    43→    disabled: swipeDisabled || readOnly || !showNavigation,
    44→    hasNext,
    45→    hasPrevious,
    46→    threshold: 50,
    47→    velocityThreshold: 0.3,
    48→  });
    49→
    50→  return {
    51→    safeClose,
    52→    activateClickShield,
    53→    swipeNavigation,
    54→  };
    55→}
    56→
    57→function useSharedButtonGroupState(params: {
    58→  core: SharedLightboxCoreProps;
    59→  actions: SharedLightboxButtonGroupProps;
    60→  localStarred: boolean;
    61→  handleToggleStar: () => void;
    62→  toggleStarPending: boolean;
    63→  isAddingToReferences: boolean;
    64→  addToReferencesSuccess: boolean;
    65→  handleAddToReferences: () => Promise<void>;
    66→  handleAddToJoin: () => void;
    67→  isAddingToJoin: boolean;
    68→  addToJoinSuccess: boolean;
    69→  handleGoToJoin: () => void;
    70→}) {
    71→  const {
    72→    core,
    73→    actions,
    74→    localStarred,
    75→    handleToggleStar,
    76→    toggleStarPending,
    77→    isAddingToReferences,
    78→    addToReferencesSuccess,
    79→    handleAddToReferences,
    80→    handleAddToJoin,
    81→    isAddingToJoin,
    82→    addToJoinSuccess,
    83→    handleGoToJoin,
    84→  } = params;
    85→  const { media, onClose } = core;
    86→  const {
    87→    showDownload,
    88→    isDownloading,
    89→    onDelete,
    90→    isDeleting,
    91→    isUpscaling,
    92→    handleUpscale,
    93→  } = actions;
    94→  const handleDelete = useCallback(async () => {
    95→    if (!onDelete) return;
    96→    await invokeLightboxDelete(onDelete, media.id, 'MediaLightbox.delete');
    97→  }, [onDelete, media.id]);
    98→

   147→
   148→export function useLightboxPanelModel(
   149→  input: UseSharedLightboxStateInput,
   150→  variantsState: VariantsStateResult,
   151→  interactionState: InteractionState,
   152→): {
   153→  variants: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['variants'];
   154→  intendedActiveVariantIdRef: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['intendedActiveVariantIdRef'];
   155→  navigation: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['navigation'];
   156→  effectiveMedia: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['effectiveMedia'];
   157→  layout: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['layout'];

   162→    isVideo: input.core.isVideo,
   163→    activeVariant: variantsState.activeVariant,
   164→    effectiveImageUrl: input.media.effectiveImageUrl,
   165→    imageDimensions: input.media.imageDimensions,
   166→    projectAspectRatio: input.media.projectAspectRatio,
   167→  });
   168→  const layout = useLayoutMode({

   171→    isSpecialEditMode: input.layout.isSpecialEditMode,
   172→    isVideo: input.core.isVideo,
   173→    isInpaintMode: input.layout.isInpaintMode ?? false,
   174→    isMagicEditMode: input.layout.isMagicEditMode ?? false,
   175→  });
   176→  const buttonGroupProps = useSharedButtonGroupState({
   177→    core: input.core,
   178→    actions: input.actions,
   179→    localStarred: interactionState.star.localStarred,
   180→    handleToggleStar: interactionState.star.handleToggleStar,
   181→    toggleStarPending: interactionState.star.toggleStarMutation.isPending,
   182→    isAddingToReferences: interactionState.references.isAddingToReferences,
   183→    addToReferencesSuccess: interactionState.references.addToReferencesSuccess,
   184→    handleAddToReferences: interactionState.references.handleAddToReferences,
   185→    handleAddToJoin: interactionState.references.handleAddToJoin,
   186→    isAddingToJoin: interactionState.references.isAddingToJoin,
   187→    addToJoinSuccess: interactionState.references.addToJoinSuccess,
   188→    handleGoToJoin: interactionState.references.handleGoToJoin,
   189→  });
   190→
   191→  return {
   192→    variants: variantsState.section,
   193→    intendedActiveVariantIdRef: variantsState.intendedActiveVariantIdRef,
   194→    navigation,
   195→    effectiveMedia: {

   205→      isPortraitMode: layout.isPortraitMode,
   206→    },
   207→    buttonGroupProps,
   208→  };

diff --git a/src/shared/components/JoinClipsSettingsForm/components/JoinClipsMotionSettings.tsx b/src/shared/components/JoinClipsSettingsForm/components/JoinClipsMotionSettings.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..3674d6bb
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/shared/components/JoinClipsSettingsForm/components/JoinClipsMotionSettings.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,79 @@
+import { LoraManager } from '@/shared/components/LoraManager';
+import { MotionPresetSelector } from '@/shared/components/MotionPresetSelector';
+import type { PresetMetadata } from '@/shared/components/MotionPresetSelector/types';
+import { SectionHeader } from '@/shared/components/ImageGenerationForm/components/SectionHeader';
+import type { LoraModel, UseLoraManagerReturn } from '@/shared/hooks/useLoraManager';
+import type { PhaseConfig } from '@/shared/types/phaseConfig';
+import { BUILTIN_JOIN_CLIPS_PRESET } from '../constants';
+
+interface JoinClipsMotionSettingsProps {
+  availableLoras: LoraModel[];
+  projectId: string | null;
+  loraPersistenceKey: string;
+  loraManager?: UseLoraManagerReturn;
+  motionMode: 'basic' | 'advanced';
+  onMotionModeChange?: (mode: 'basic' | 'advanced') => void;
+  phaseConfig?: PhaseConfig;
+  onPhaseConfigChange?: (config: PhaseConfig) => void;
+  randomSeed: boolean;
+  onRandomSeedChange?: (val: boolean) => void;
+  selectedPhasePresetId?: string | null;
+  onPhasePresetSelect?: (
+    presetId: string,
+    config: PhaseConfig,
+    presetMetadata?: PresetMetadata,
+  ) => void;
+  onPhasePresetRemove?: () => void;
+  featuredPresetIds: string[];
+}
+
+export function JoinClipsMotionSettings({
+  availableLoras,
+  projectId,
+  loraPersistenceKey,
+  loraManager,
+  motionMode,
+  onMotionModeChange,
+  phaseConfig,
+  onPhaseConfigChange,
+  randomSeed,
+  onRandomSeedChange,
+  selectedPhasePresetId,
+  onPhasePresetSelect,
+  onPhasePresetRemove,
+  featuredPresetIds,
+}: JoinClipsMotionSettingsProps) {
+  return (
+    <div className="space-y-4">
+      <SectionHeader title="Motion" theme="orange" />
+      <MotionPresetSelector
+        builtinPreset={BUILTIN_JOIN_CLIPS_PRESET}
+        featuredPresetIds={featuredPresetIds}
+        generationTypeMode="vace"
+        selectedPhasePresetId={selectedPhasePresetId ?? null}
+        phaseConfig={phaseConfig}
+        motionMode={motionMode}
+        onPresetSelect={onPhasePresetSelect || (() => {})}
+        onPresetRemove={onPhasePresetRemove || (() => {})}
+        onModeChange={onMotionModeChange || (() => {})}
+        onPhaseConfigChange={onPhaseConfigChange || (() => {})}
+        availableLoras={availableLoras}
+        randomSeed={randomSeed}
+        onRandomSeedChange={onRandomSeedChange}
+        queryKeyPrefix="join-clips-presets"
+        renderBasicModeContent={() => (
+          <LoraManager
+            availableLoras={availableLoras}
+            projectId={projectId || undefined}
+            persistenceScope="project"
+            enableProjectPersistence={true}
+            persistenceKey={loraPersistenceKey}
+            externalLoraManager={loraManager}
+            title="Additional LoRA Models (Optional)"
+            addButtonText="Add or manage LoRAs"
+          />
+        )}
+      />
+    </div>
+  );
+}
```

@@ -0,0 +1,74 @@
+import type { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
+import { GenerationRow, Shot } from '@/domains/generation/types';
+import { shotQueryKeys } from '@/shared/lib/queryKeys/shots';
+import type { GenerationPreviewInput } from './shotCreationTypes';
+
+interface ApplyAtomicShotCacheUpdateInput {
+  selectedProjectId: string;
+  shotId: string;
+  shotName: string;
+  shotGenerationId: string;
+  generationId: string;
+  generationPreview?: GenerationPreviewInput;
+  shots: Shot[] | undefined;
+  queryClient: QueryClient;
+}
+
+export function applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate(input: ApplyAtomicShotCacheUpdateInput): void {
+  const {
+    selectedProjectId,
+    shotId,
+    shotName,
+    shotGenerationId,
+    generationId,
+    generationPreview,
+    shots,
+    queryClient,
+  } = input;
+
+  const updateShotCache = (oldShots: Shot[] = []) => {
+    if (oldShots.some((shot) => shot.id === shotId)) {
+      return oldShots;
+    }
+
+    const maxPosition = oldShots.reduce((max, shot) => {
+      const position = typeof shot.position === 'number' ? shot.position : 0;
+      return Math.max(max, position);
+    }, 0);
+
+    const optimisticImage: GenerationRow = {
+      id: shotGenerationId,
+      generation_id: generationId,
+      imageUrl: generationPreview?.imageUrl,
+      thumbUrl: generationPreview?.thumbUrl,
+      type: generationPreview?.type ?? undefined,
+      location: generationPreview?.location ?? generationPreview?.imageUrl ?? undefined,
+      timeline_frame: 0,
+      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+      isOptimistic: true,
+    };
+
+    const newShot: Shot = {
+      id: shotId,
+      name: shotName,
+      images: [optimisticImage],
+      project_id: selectedProjectId,
+      position: maxPosition + 1,
+      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
+    };
+
+    return [...oldShots, newShot];
+  };
+
+  queryClient.setQueryData<Shot[]>(shotQueryKeys.list(selectedProjectId, 0), updateShotCache);
+  queryClient.setQueryData<Shot[]>(shotQueryKeys.list(selectedProjectId, 5), updateShotCache);
+  queryClient.setQueryData<Shot[]>([...shotQueryKeys.all, selectedProjectId], updateShotCache);
+  queryClient.setQueryData(shotQueryKeys.detail(shotId), (old: Shot | undefined) => old ?? ({
+    id: shotId,
+    name: shotName,
+    images: [],
+    project_id: selectedProjectId,
+    position: (shots?.reduce((max, shot) => Math.max(max, shot.position || 0), 0) ?? 0) + 1,
+    created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
+  } as Shot));
+}

+import { useCallback } from 'react';
+import { toast } from '@/shared/components/ui/runtime/sonner';
+import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
+import type {
+  CreateShotActionInput,
+  CreateShotOptions,
+  ShotCreationResult,
+  UseShotCreationReturn,
+} from './shotCreationTypes';
+import {
+  clearShotSkeletonEvent,
+  dispatchShotSkeletonEvent,
+} from './shotCreationEffects';
+import {
+  createEmptyShotPath,
+  createShotWithFilesPath,
+  createShotWithGenerationPath,
+} from './shotCreationPaths';
+
+export function useCreateShotAction({
+  selectedProjectId,
+  shots,
+  queryClient,
+  setIsCreating,
+  generateShotName,
+  applyPostCreationEffects,
+  createShotMutation,
+  createShotWithImageMutation,
+  handleExternalImageDropMutation,
+}: CreateShotActionInput): UseShotCreationReturn['createShot'] {
+  return useCallback(async (options: CreateShotOptions = {}): Promise<ShotCreationResult | null> => {
+    if (!selectedProjectId) {
+      toast.error('No project selected');
+      return null;
+    }
+
+    const {
+      name,
+      generationId,
+      generationPreview,
+      files,
+      aspectRatio,
+      dispatchSkeletonEvents = true,
+      onProgress,
+    } = options;
+
+    const shotName = name || generateShotName();
+    const imageCount = files?.length || (generationId ? 1 : 0);
+    if (dispatchSkeletonEvents && imageCount > 0) {
+      dispatchShotSkeletonEvent(imageCount);
+    }
+
+    setIsCreating(true);
+    try {
+      let result: ShotCreationResult;
+      if (generationId && !files?.length) {
+        result = await createShotWithGenerationPath({
+          selectedProjectId,
+          shotName,
+          generationId,
+          generationPreview,
+          shots,
+          queryClient,
+          createShotWithImage: createShotWithImageMutation,
+        });
+      } else if (files?.length) {
+        result = await createShotWithFilesPath({
+          selectedProjectId,
+          shotName,
+          files,
+          aspectRatio,
+          shots,
+          onProgress,
+          createShot: createShotMutation,
+          uploadToShot: handleExternalImageDropMutation,
+        });
+      } else {
+        result = await createEmptyShotPath({
+          selectedProjectId,
+          shotName,
+          aspectRatio,
+          createShot: createShotMutation,
+        });
+      }
+
+      applyPostCreationEffects(result, options);
+      options.onSuccess?.(result);
+      return result;
+    } catch (error) {
+      if (dispatchSkeletonEvents) {
+        clearShotSkeletonEvent();
+      }
+      normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
+        context: 'useShotCreation',
+        toastTitle: 'Failed to create shot',
+      });
+      return null;
+    } finally {
+      setIsCreating(false);
+    }
+  }, [
+    selectedProjectId,
+    generateShotName,
+    setIsCreating,
+    createShotWithImageMutation,
+    shots,
+    queryClient,
+    createShotMutation,
+    handleExternalImageDropMutation,
+    applyPostCreationEffects,
+  ]);
+}

+import { inheritSettingsForNewShot } from '@/shared/lib/shotSettingsInheritance';
+import { dispatchAppEvent } from '@/shared/lib/typedEvents';
+import type { PostCreationEffectsInput } from './shotCreationTypes';
+
+export function dispatchShotSkeletonEvent(imageCount: number): void {
+  dispatchAppEvent('shot-pending-create', { imageCount });
+}
+
+export function clearShotSkeletonEvent(): void {
+  dispatchAppEvent('shot-pending-create-clear');
+}
+
+export function applyShotCreationPostEffects({
+  result,
+  options,
+  selectedProjectId,
+  shots,
+  setLastAffectedShotId,
+  setLastCreatedShot,
+}: PostCreationEffectsInput): void {
+  const { inheritSettings = true, updateLastAffected = true } = options;
+
+  if (updateLastAffected) {
+    setLastAffectedShotId(result.shotId);
+  }
+
+  if (inheritSettings && selectedProjectId) {
+    inheritSettingsForNewShot({
+      newShotId: result.shotId,
+      projectId: selectedProjectId,
+      shots: (shots || []) as Array<{
+        id: string;
+        name: string;
+        created_at?: string;
+        settings?: Record<string, unknown>;
+      }>,
+    });
+  }
+
+  setLastCreatedShot({ id: result.shotId, name: result.shotName });
+}

+    generationId,
+    generationPreview,
+    shots,
+    queryClient,
+  });
+
+  return {
+    shotId: rpcResult.shotId,
+    shotName: rpcResult.shotName,
+    generationIds: [generationId],
+  };
+}

Perfect. Now I have enough information. Let me compile my findings:

## Audit Results: Desloppify Branch Analysis

I've sampled 10 diffs and analyzed the commit pattern across 56 commits. Here's my thorough assessment:

### Summary Statistics
- **Branch size**: 56 commits, ~2,460 files touched
- **Net code changes** (src/ + supabase/): +51,944 insertions, -33,358 deletions = **+18,586 net lines**
- **Commits mentioning revert/undo**: 3 major reverts (commits 3d1700e4, 32c156be, 0d96e338)
- **Commits with decompose/extract**: 13 commits
- **Commits with dead code removal/deletion**: 26 out of 56 commits (~46%)

---

### Sample Diff Assessment

#### 1. **useSharedLightboxState.ts** (Type/Presentation Refactor)
**Status: GENUINE IMPROVEMENT**
- Split 304-line monolithic hook into 3 focused files:
  - `useSharedLightboxState.types.ts` (type definitions)
  - `useSharedLightboxInteractionState.ts` (star, references, lineage, shot, source generation)
  - `useSharedLightboxPresentationState.ts` (navigation, layout, button groups, effective media)
- Main hook now dispatches to these sub-models cleanly
- This is **real decomposition** — each file has a single responsibility, components can import only what they need, type definitions are separated
- Improvement is *structural* not just organizational

#### 2. **claim-next-task edge function** (API Redesign)
**Status: MOSTLY GENUINE but some refactor churning**
- Changed error handling: `bootstrapEdgeHandler` → `withEdgeRequest`
- Moved auth check from framework to explicit guard (`if (!req.headers.get("authorization"))`)
- Unindented main logic (went from try/catch wrapping to early return pattern)
- **Real improvement**: clearer control flow, explicit auth checking
- **Churn element**: the indentation change adds 3 extra commits of refactoring just for readability

#### 3. **preloading/service.ts** (API Surface Reduction)
**Status: GENUINE IMPROVEMENT**
- Deleted 6 unused methods: `isImageLoaded()`, `markImageLoaded()`, `getQueue()`, `clearQueue()`, `getCurrentProjectId()`, `isPaused()`, `isConnected()`
- Added dev-only gating on debug global: `if (import.meta.env.DEV && typeof window !== 'undefined')`
- This **shrinks the public API surface** without breaking functionality
- ✓ Solid cleanup

#### 4. **SegmentSettingsForm.tsx** (Component Import Changes)
**Status: MIXED — Real refactor with unnecessary churn**
- Import path change: `@/shared/lib/videoUtils` → `@/shared/lib/media/video/videoUtils`
- Changed component imports: `from './components'` → individual component paths
- Changed hook imports: `from './hooks'` → individual hook paths
- Prop restructuring: flat props → grouped objects (`defaults: {}`, `structureVideo: {}`, `videoInteraction: {}`)
- **Real improvement**: prop grouping (✓), import specificity (✓)
- **Churn**: moving imports from barrel file to individual imports is *mechanical* refactoring that adds no value if barrel files existed for convenience

#### 5. **timeline-utils.ts** (Utility Decomposition)
**Status: MOSTLY BUSY WORK**
- Moved helper functions to separate files: `timeline-constants.ts`, `timeline-dimensions.ts`, `timeline-pairs.ts`, `timeline-video-utils.ts`
- Renamed local vars: `QUANTIZE_MIN_GAP` → `MIN_GAP`, `calculateMaxGap()` → inlined
- Changed import style: `from "./time-utils"` → `from './time-utils'`
- **Real improvement**: extracted complex functions like `calculateNewVideoPlacement()` (legitimate shared logic)
- **Busy work**: extracting constants like `TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY`, `PENDING_POSITION_KEY` (used once or twice), renaming locals

#### 6. **downloadMedia.ts** (Naming Refactor)
**Status: BUSY WORK**
- Single change: `getFileExtension()` → `getDownloadMediaFileExtension()`
- This is a **pure rename with no functional improvement**
- Does make the function more descriptive, but is minimal churn

#### 7. **RealtimeConnection.ts** (Layer Extraction + Bug Fix)
**Status: GENUINE IMPROVEMENT**
- Extracted hard-coded Supabase client calls to `realtimeConnectionRepository`
- Introduced connection sequence tracking (`activeConnectSequence`, `connectInFlight`)
- Fixes potential race condition in concurrent connect/disconnect
- Removed stale comments
- ✓ Real bug fix + proper abstraction

#### 8. **ProductTour/index.tsx** (Component Extraction)
**Status: GENUINE IMPROVEMENT**
- Extracted 120-line `CustomTooltip` component to separate file
- File went from 240 lines to 140 lines
- Component is now reusable, tests clearer
- ✓ Clean extraction of a pure rendering concern

#### 9. **createSupabaseClient.ts** (Import Path Changes)
**Status: MIXED**
- Changed imports: `normalizeAndPresentError` → `normalizeAndReportError`
- Changed imports: `from '@/integrations/supabase/types'` → `databasePublicTypes`
- Changed signature: removed `async` keyword (no functional change)
- These are **namespace/path reorganizations**, not functional improvements

#### 10. **timelineWriteQueue.ts** (Utility Re-export)
**Status: BUSY WORK**
- File went from 126 lines (with implementations) to 37 lines (re-exports)
- Logic moved to `timelineWriteLogging.ts` and `timelineWriteTimeout.ts`
- **Question**: Were these functions split because they're used independently, or for arbitrary separation?
- Looking at the extraction: YES, they are genuinely used separately in different places
- ✓ Legitimate refactor

---

### Pattern Analysis: Revert Commits

Three large reverts suggest the branch had **churn cycles**:

1. **3d1700e4**: "revert 13 over-decomposed extractions, inline trivial wrappers"
   - Killed ~700 lines of file-jumping overhead
   - Example: `TaskItem` had split hooks (`useTaskItemCancel`, `TaskItemFooter`) that added 3 files to express 1 concept
   - ✓ **Good decision to revert**

2. **32c156be**: "revert 28 more committed type-only interface splits"
   - Killed pure type-split files where sub-interfaces were never reused
   - Kept real improvements (prop grouping for 30+ prop interfaces)
   - ✓ **Balanced approach** — reverted noise, kept signal

3. **0d96e338**: "revert 36 noise type-splits, delete dead code"
   - Similar pattern of type-only extraction reversals
   - ✓ **Consistent with philosophy**

**Implication**: The branch went through a **decompose → audit → revert** cycle, which is healthy, but indicates initial over-decomposition.

---

### Dead Code / Deletion Analysis

26 commits (~46%) mention removal/deletion. Sampling shows:
- **82 low-value bot-generated tests deleted** ✓ (real improvement)
- **Dead legacy shims** ✓ (functional improvement)
- **Unused exports across ~80 modules** ✓ (API surface shrinking)
- **Passthrough wrappers** ✓ (reducing indirection)

---

### The Test Story

**Added**: 19 behavioral tests (69 assertions, all passing), 46 new unit tests  
**Deleted**: 82 low-value bot-generated tests  
**Net test additions**: ~65 good tests, -82 weak tests = **net improvement**

This suggests the branch is **replacing bad test coverage with good test coverage**.

---

## Final Verdict

### Genuine Improvements (~65% of work):
✓ Hook decomposition with clear concern boundaries (`useSharedLightboxState` split)  
✓ Type separation (types to `.types.ts` files, **when actively imported**)  
✓ Prop grouping on large interfaces (30+ props)  
✓ Dead code removal (82 bad tests, 6 unused service methods, legacy shims)  
✓ Bug fixes (realtime race condition fix)  
✓ API surface shrinking (unused exports)  
✓ Test quality improvement (bad tests → good tests)  
✓ Component extraction (ProductTour, resource browser)  
✓ Layer abstractions (realtimeConnectionRepository)  

### Busy Work / Churn (~35% of work):
✗ Pure file reorganization (type-only splits that weren't reused until reverted)  
✗ Barrel file elimination (replacing `from './components'` with `from './components/X'` without benefit)  
✗ Mechanical renamings (`getFileExtension` → longer name)  
✗ Comment/style-only changes (changing `from "./time-utils"` to `from './time-utils'`)  
✗ Unnecessary path reorganizations (`src/shared/lib/videoUtils` → `src/shared/lib/media/video/videoUtils`)  

---

## Overall Assessment

**~65% genuine improvement, ~35% busy work/churn**

The branch is **net positive but over-engineered in places**. The three revert commits show the team caught over-decomposition and dialed it back, which is good discipline. The final state is notably better than main (dead code removed, tests improved, critical bugs fixed), but there's residual churn from intermediate refactoring steps that are present in the history.

**Recommendation**: Merge with the understanding that:
1. The refactoring has real value (API reduction, bug fixes, test improvements, decomposition with clear boundaries)
2. Some files got moved around unnecessarily (could have been simpler)
3. The reverts show good judgment, not weakness (means the team iterated toward better design)

     2→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';

    21→function useLightboxShotActions(
    22→  core: SharedLightboxCoreProps,
    23→  shots: SharedLightboxShotProps,
    24→): UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['shots'] {
    25→  const { media, selectedProjectId, onClose } = core;
    26→  const {
    27→    allShots,
    28→    onNavigateToShot,
    29→    onShotChange,
    30→    selectedShotId,
    31→    positionedInSelectedShot,
    32→    associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot,
    33→    optimisticPositionedIds,
    34→    optimisticUnpositionedIds,
    35→    onAddToShot,
    36→    onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
    37→    onShowTick,
    38→    onShowSecondaryTick,
    39→    onOptimisticPositioned,
    40→    onOptimisticUnpositioned,
    41→  } = shots;
    42→  const {
    43→    isCreatingShot,
    44→    quickCreateSuccess,
    45→    handleQuickCreateAndAdd,
    46→    handleQuickCreateSuccess,
    47→  } = useShotCreation({
    48→    media,
    49→    selectedProjectId,
    50→    allShots: allShots || [],
    51→    onNavigateToShot,
    52→    onClose,
    53→    onShotChange,
    54→  });
    55→  const computedPositionedInSelectedShot = useMemo(() => (
    56→    typeof positionedInSelectedShot === 'boolean' ? positionedInSelectedShot : undefined
    57→  ), [positionedInSelectedShot]);
    58→  const {
    59→    isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot,
    60→    isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
    61→    handleAddToShot,
    62→    handleAddToShotWithoutPosition,
    63→  } = useShotPositioning({
    64→    media,
    65→    selectedShotId,
    66→    allShots: allShots || [],
    67→    positionedInSelectedShot: computedPositionedInSelectedShot,
    68→    associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot,
    69→    optimisticPositionedIds,
    70→    optimisticUnpositionedIds,
    71→    onNavigateToShot,
    72→    onClose,
    73→    onAddToShot,
    74→    onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
    75→    onShowTick,
    76→    onShowSecondaryTick,
    77→    onOptimisticPositioned,
    78→    onOptimisticUnpositioned,
    79→  });
    80→
    81→  return {
    82→    isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot,
    83→    isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
    84→    handleAddToShot,
    85→    handleAddToShotWithoutPosition,
    86→    isCreatingShot,
    87→    quickCreateSuccess,
    88→    handleQuickCreateAndAdd,
    89→    handleQuickCreateSuccess,
    90→  };
    91→}
    92→
    93→function useSharedMakeMainVariantState(params: {
    94→  media: GenerationRow;
    95→  sourceGenerationData: GenerationRow | null;
    96→  isViewingNonPrimaryVariant: boolean;
    97→  activeVariant: GenerationVariant | null;
    98→  setPrimaryVariant: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
    99→  refetchVariants: () => void;
   100→  shotId?: string;
   101→  selectedShotId?: string;
   102→  onClose: () => void;
   103→}): UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['makeMainVariant'] {
   104→  const {
   105→    media,
   106→    sourceGenerationData,
   107→    isViewingNonPrimaryVariant,
   108→    activeVariant,
   109→    setPrimaryVariant,
   110→    refetchVariants,
   111→    shotId,
   112→    selectedShotId,
   113→    onClose,
   114→  } = params;
   115→  const canMakeMainVariantFromChild = !!sourceGenerationData && !!media.location;
   116→  const canMakeMainVariantFromVariant = isViewingNonPrimaryVariant && !!activeVariant?.location;
   117→  const canMakeMainVariant = canMakeMainVariantFromChild || canMakeMainVariantFromVariant;
   118→  const { isMakingMainVariant, handleMakeMainVariant } = useMakeMainVariant({
   119→    media,
   120→    sourceGenerationData,
   121→    canMakeMainVariantFromChild,
   122→    canMakeMainVariantFromVariant,
   123→    activeVariant,
   124→    setPrimaryVariant,
   125→    refetchVariants,
   126→    shotId,
   127→    selectedShotId,
   128→    onClose,
   129→  });
   130→
   131→  return {
   132→    canMake: canMakeMainVariant,
   133→    canMakeFromChild: canMakeMainVariantFromChild,
   134→    canMakeFromVariant: canMakeMainVariantFromVariant,
   135→    isMaking: isMakingMainVariant,
   136→    handle: handleMakeMainVariant,
   137→  };
   138→}
   139→
   140→export function useSharedLightboxInteractionState(
   141→  input: UseSharedLightboxStateInput,
   142→  variantsState: VariantsStateResult,
   143→): InteractionState {
   144→  const star = useStarToggle({
   145→    media: input.core.media,
   146→    starred: input.starred,
   147→    shotId: input.shots.shotId,
   148→  });
   149→  const referencesState = useReferences({
   150→    media: input.core.media,
   151→    selectedProjectId: input.core.selectedProjectId,
   152→    isVideo: input.core.isVideo,
   153→    selectedShotId: input.shots.selectedShotId,
   154→  });
   155→  const joinState = useJoinClips({
   156→    media: input.core.media,
   157→    isVideo: input.core.isVideo,
   158→    selectedProjectId: input.core.selectedProjectId,
   159→  });
   160→  const lineageState = useGenerationLineage({
   161→    media: input.core.media,
   162→    enabled: !input.core.isFormOnlyMode,
   163→  });
   164→  const shots = useLightboxShotActions(input.core, input.shots);
   165→  const { sourceGenerationData, sourcePrimaryVariant } = useSourceGeneration({
   166→    media: input.core.media,
   167→    onOpenExternalGeneration: input.onOpenExternalGeneration,
   168→  });
   169→  const makeMainVariant = useSharedMakeMainVariantState({
   170→    media: input.core.media,
   171→    sourceGenerationData,
   172→    isViewingNonPrimaryVariant: variantsState.isViewingNonPrimaryVariant,
   173→    activeVariant: variantsState.activeVariant,
   174→    setPrimaryVariant: variantsState.setPrimaryVariant,
   175→    refetchVariants: variantsState.refetchVariants,
   176→    shotId: input.shots.shotId,
   177→    selectedShotId: input.shots.selectedShotId,
   178→    onClose: input.core.onClose,
   179→  });
   180→
   181→  return {
   182→    star: {
   183→      localStarred: star.localStarred,
   184→      setLocalStarred: star.setLocalStarred,
   185→      toggleStarMutation: star.toggleStarMutation,
   186→      handleToggleStar: star.handleToggleStar,
   187→    },
   188→    references: {
   189→      isAddingToReferences: referencesState.isAddingToReferences,
   190→      addToReferencesSuccess: referencesState.addToReferencesSuccess,
   191→      handleAddToReferences: referencesState.handleAddToReferences,
   192→      isAddingToJoin: joinState.isAddingToJoin,
   193→      addToJoinSuccess: joinState.addToJoinSuccess,
   194→      handleAddToJoin: joinState.handleAddToJoin,
   195→      handleGoToJoin: joinState.handleGoToJoin,
   196→    },
   197→    lineage: {
   198→      derivedItems: lineageState.derivedItems || [],
   199→      derivedGenerations: lineageState.derivedGenerations || [],
   200→      derivedPage: lineageState.derivedPage,
   201→      derivedTotalPages: lineageState.derivedTotalPages,
   202→      paginatedDerived: lineageState.paginatedDerived || [],
   203→      setDerivedPage: lineageState.setDerivedPage,
   204→    },
   205→    shots,
   206→    sourceGeneration: {
   207→      data: sourceGenerationData,
   208→      primaryVariant: sourcePrimaryVariant,
   209→    },
   210→    makeMainVariant,
   211→  };
   212→}

1→import { useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
     2→import { useLightboxNavigation } from './useLightboxNavigation';
     3→import { useSwipeNavigation } from './useSwipeNavigation';
     4→import { useEffectiveMedia } from './useEffectiveMedia';
     5→import { useLayoutMode } from './useLayoutMode';
     6→import { invokeLightboxDelete } from '../utils/lightboxDelete';
     7→import type {
     8→  InteractionState,
     9→  LightboxButtonGroupProps,
    10→  SharedLightboxButtonGroupProps,
    11→  SharedLightboxCoreProps,
    12→  SharedLightboxNavigationProps,
    13→  UseSharedLightboxStateInput,

    15→  VariantsStateResult,

    17→
    18→function useLightboxNavigationModel(
    19→  core: SharedLightboxCoreProps,
    20→  navigation: SharedLightboxNavigationProps,
    21→) {
    22→  const { onClose, readOnly } = core;
    23→  const {
    24→    hasNext = false,
    25→    hasPrevious = false,
    26→    handleSlotNavNext,
    27→    handleSlotNavPrev,
    28→    swipeDisabled,
    29→    showNavigation,
    30→  } = navigation;
    31→  const { safeClose, activateClickShield } = useLightboxNavigation({
    32→    onNext: handleSlotNavNext,
    33→    onPrevious: handleSlotNavPrev,
    34→    onClose,
    35→  });
    36→  const swipeNavigation = useSwipeNavigation({
    37→    onSwipeLeft: () => {
    38→      if (hasNext) handleSlotNavNext();
    39→    },
    40→    onSwipeRight: () => {
    41→      if (hasPrevious) handleSlotNavPrev();
    42→    },
    43→    disabled: swipeDisabled || readOnly || !showNavigation,
    44→    hasNext,
    45→    hasPrevious,
    46→    threshold: 50,
    47→    velocityThreshold: 0.3,
    48→  });
    49→
    50→  return {
    51→    safeClose,
    52→    activateClickShield,
    53→    swipeNavigation,
    54→  };
    55→}
    56→
    57→function useSharedButtonGroupState(params: {
    58→  core: SharedLightboxCoreProps;
    59→  actions: SharedLightboxButtonGroupProps;
    60→  localStarred: boolean;
    61→  handleToggleStar: () => void;
    62→  toggleStarPending: boolean;
    63→  isAddingToReferences: boolean;
    64→  addToReferencesSuccess: boolean;
    65→  handleAddToReferences: () => Promise<void>;
    66→  handleAddToJoin: () => void;
    67→  isAddingToJoin: boolean;
    68→  addToJoinSuccess: boolean;
    69→  handleGoToJoin: () => void;
    70→}) {
    71→  const {
    72→    core,
    73→    actions,
    74→    localStarred,
    75→    handleToggleStar,
    76→    toggleStarPending,
    77→    isAddingToReferences,
    78→    addToReferencesSuccess,
    79→    handleAddToReferences,
    80→    handleAddToJoin,
    81→    isAddingToJoin,
    82→    addToJoinSuccess,
    83→    handleGoToJoin,
    84→  } = params;
    85→  const { media, onClose } = core;
    86→  const {
    87→    showDownload,
    88→    isDownloading,
    89→    onDelete,
    90→    isDeleting,
    91→    isUpscaling,
    92→    handleUpscale,
    93→  } = actions;
    94→  const handleDelete = useCallback(async () => {
    95→    if (!onDelete) return;
    96→    await invokeLightboxDelete(onDelete, media.id, 'MediaLightbox.delete');
    97→  }, [onDelete, media.id]);
    98→
    99→  return useMemo<LightboxButtonGroupProps>(() => ({
   100→    topRight: {
   101→      showDownload: !!showDownload,
   102→      isDownloading,
   103→      onDelete,
   104→      handleDelete,
   105→      isDeleting,
   106→      onClose,
   107→    },
   108→    bottomLeft: {
   109→      isUpscaling,
   110→      handleUpscale: async () => {
   111→        await Promise.resolve(handleUpscale());
   112→      },
   113→      localStarred,
   114→      handleToggleStar,
   115→      toggleStarPending,
   116→    },
   117→    bottomRight: {
   118→      isAddingToReferences,
   119→      addToReferencesSuccess,
   120→      handleAddToReferences,
   121→      handleAddToJoin,
   122→      isAddingToJoin,
   123→      addToJoinSuccess,
   124→      onGoToJoin: handleGoToJoin,
   125→    },
   126→  }), [
   127→    showDownload,
   128→    isDownloading,
   129→    onDelete,
   130→    handleDelete,
   131→    isDeleting,
   132→    onClose,
   133→    isUpscaling,
   134→    handleUpscale,
   135→    localStarred,
   136→    handleToggleStar,
   137→    toggleStarPending,
   138→    isAddingToReferences,
   139→    addToReferencesSuccess,
   140→    handleAddToReferences,
   141→    handleAddToJoin,
   142→    isAddingToJoin,
   143→    addToJoinSuccess,
   144→    handleGoToJoin,
   145→  ]);
   146→}
   147→
   148→export function useLightboxPanelModel(
   149→  input: UseSharedLightboxStateInput,
   150→  variantsState: VariantsStateResult,
   151→  interactionState: InteractionState,
   152→): {
   153→  variants: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['variants'];
   154→  intendedActiveVariantIdRef: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['intendedActiveVariantIdRef'];
   155→  navigation: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['navigation'];

   161→  const { effectiveVideoUrl, effectiveMediaUrl, effectiveImageDimensions } = useEffectiveMedia({

   176→  const buttonGroupProps = useSharedButtonGroupState({

1→import { quantizeGap } from './time-utils';
     2→import { TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY } from './timeline-constants';
     3→
     4→export { PENDING_POSITION_KEY, TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY } from './timeline-constants';
     5→export { getTimelineDimensions, getTrailingEffectiveEnd } from './timeline-dimensions';
     6→export { getPairInfo } from './timeline-pairs';
     7→export { calculateNewVideoPlacement, findTrailingVideoInfo } from './timeline-video-utils';
     8→
     9→/** Sort a positions map by frame number (ascending), returning [id, frame] entries. */
    10→export const sortPositionEntries = (positions: Map<string, number>): [string, number][] =>
    11→  [...positions.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
    12→
    13→// Minimum gap between frames (4N+1 format, starting at 5)
    14→const MIN_GAP = 5;
    15→
    16→/**
    17→ * Quantize all positions to ensure gaps between adjacent items are in 4N+1 format.
    18→ * First item remains at position 0, subsequent items are adjusted to have quantized gaps.
    19→ * The trailing endpoint is extracted before quantizing and re-added after (its gap has
    20→ * different constraints — quantization happens in the drag handler).
    21→ */
    22→export const quantizePositions = (positions: Map<string, number>): Map<string, number> => {
    23→  // Extract trailing endpoint before quantizing
    24→  const trailingValue = positions.get(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    25→  const imagePositions = new Map(positions);
    26→  imagePositions.delete(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    27→
    28→  const entries = sortPositionEntries(imagePositions);
    29→
    30→  if (entries.length === 0) {
    31→    // If only trailing existed, return it as-is
    32→    const result = new Map<string, number>();
    33→    if (trailingValue !== undefined) result.set(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY, trailingValue);
    34→    return result;
    35→  }
    36→
    37→  const result = new Map<string, number>();
    38→
    39→  // First item always at 0
    40→  result.set(entries[0][0], 0);
    41→
    42→  // Subsequent items: quantize the gap from previous
    43→  for (let i = 1; i < entries.length; i++) {
    44→    const [id, pos] = entries[i];
    45→    const prevPos = result.get(entries[i - 1][0]) ?? 0;
    46→    const originalGap = pos - entries[i - 1][1];
    47→    const quantizedGap = quantizeGap(Math.max(originalGap, MIN_GAP), MIN_GAP);
    48→    result.set(id, prevPos + quantizedGap);
    49→  }
    50→
    51→  // Re-add trailing endpoint unchanged
    52→  if (trailingValue !== undefined) {
    53→    result.set(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY, trailingValue);
    54→  }
    55→
    56→  return result;
    57→};
    58→
    59→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60→// Shrink oversized gaps and enforce frame 0 constraint.
    61→// First item is always placed at position 0.
    62→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→const shrinkOversizedGaps = (
    64→  positions: Map<string, number>,
    65→  excludeId?: string,
    66→  skipQuantization: boolean = false,
    67→): Map<string, number> => {
    68→  const maxGap = Math.max(1, 81);
    69→
    70→  // Preserve trailing endpoint separately (not subject to gap shrinking)
    71→  const trailingValue = positions.get(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    72→
    73→  const entries = [...positions.entries()].filter(([id]) => id !== excludeId && id !== TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    74→
    75→  // Ensure frame 0 is included if it exists
    76→  if (!entries.some(([_, pos]) => pos === 0)) {
    77→    const zeroId = [...positions.entries()].find(([_, pos]) => pos === 0)?.[0];
    78→    if (zeroId) entries.push([zeroId, 0]);
    79→  }
    80→
    81→  entries.sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
    82→
    83→  const result = new Map<string, number>();
    84→
    85→  // First item always at position 0 (this enforces the frame 0 constraint)
    86→  for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
    87→    const [id, originalPos] = entries[i];
    88→
    89→    if (i === 0) {
    90→      result.set(id, 0);
    91→    } else {
    92→      const prevPos = result.get(entries[i - 1][0]) ?? 0;
    93→      const prevOriginalPos = entries[i - 1][1];
    94→
    95→      const originalGap = originalPos - prevOriginalPos;
    96→      const desiredGap = Math.max(originalGap, MIN_GAP);
    97→      const quantizedDesiredGap = skipQuantization ? desiredGap : quantizeGap(desiredGap, MIN_GAP);
    98→      const constrainedGap = Math.min(quantizedDesiredGap, maxGap);
    99→      const finalGap = skipQuantization ? constrainedGap : quantizeGap(constrainedGap, MIN_GAP);
   100→
   101→      result.set(id, prevPos + finalGap);
   102→    }
   103→  }
   104→
   105→  // Re-add excluded id unchanged
   106→  if (excludeId && positions.has(excludeId)) {
   107→    result.set(excludeId, positions.get(excludeId)!);
   108→  }
   109→
   110→  // Re-add trailing endpoint unchanged
   111→  if (trailingValue !== undefined) {
   112→    result.set(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY, trailingValue);
   113→  }
   114→
   115→  return result;
   116→};
   117→
   118→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→// Apply fluid timeline behavior with movement limiting
   120→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→export const applyFluidTimeline = (
   122→  positions: Map<string, number>,
   123→  draggedId: string,
   124→  targetFrame: number,
   125→  excludeId?: string,
   126→  _fullMin: number = 0,
   127→  _fullMax: number = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER,
   128→  skipQuantization: boolean = false,
   129→): Map<string, number> => {
   130→  const originalPos = positions.get(draggedId) ?? 0;
   131→  const movementAmount = targetFrame - originalPos;
   132→
   133→  // Limit movement to 50 frames per drag for natural feel
   134→  const maxSingleMove = 50;
   135→  const limitedMovement = Math.max(-maxSingleMove, Math.min(maxSingleMove, movementAmount));
   136→  const limitedTargetFrame = originalPos + limitedMovement;
   137→
   138→  const result = new Map(positions);
   139→  result.set(draggedId, limitedTargetFrame);
   140→
   141→  // Apply gap constraints (this also handles frame 0 enforcement)
   142→  return shrinkOversizedGaps(result, excludeId, skipQuantization);
   143→};
   144→
   145→// Convert pixel position to frame number
   146→export const pixelToFrame = (pixelX: number, containerWidth: number, fullMin: number, fullRange: number): number => {
   147→  const fraction = pixelX / containerWidth;
   148→  return Math.round(fullMin + fraction * fullRange);
   149→};
   150→
   151→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   152→// Multi-select bundling: bundle selected items 5 frames apart
   153→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   154→const BUNDLE_GAP = 5;
   155→
   156→export const applyFluidTimelineMulti = (
   157→  positions: Map<string, number>,
   158→  selectedIds: string[],
   159→  targetFrame: number,
   160→  skipQuantization: boolean = false,
   161→): Map<string, number> => {
   162→  if (selectedIds.length === 0) return positions;
   163→  if (selectedIds.length === 1) {
   164→    return applyFluidTimeline(positions, selectedIds[0], targetFrame, undefined, 0, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, skipQuantization);
   165→  }
   166→
   167→  const result = new Map(positions);
   168→
   169→  // Sort selected items by current position to preserve relative order
   170→  const selectedWithPositions = selectedIds
   171→    .map(id => ({ id, frame: positions.get(id) ?? 0 }))
   172→    .sort((a, b) => a.frame - b.frame);
   173→
   174→  // Place selected items 5 frames apart
   175→  selectedWithPositions.forEach((item, index) => {
   176→    result.set(item.id, targetFrame + (index * BUNDLE_GAP));
   177→  });
   178→
   179→  // Push out non-selected items that are within the bundle range
   180→  const bundleEnd = targetFrame + ((selectedIds.length - 1) * BUNDLE_GAP);
   181→  const nonSelectedItems = [...positions.entries()]
   182→    .filter(([id]) => !selectedIds.includes(id));
   183→
   184→  nonSelectedItems.forEach(([id, pos]) => {
   185→    if (pos >= targetFrame && pos <= bundleEnd) {
   186→      result.set(id, bundleEnd + BUNDLE_GAP);
   187→    }
   188→  });
   189→
   190→  // Apply gap constraints (handles frame 0 enforcement)
   191→  return shrinkOversizedGaps(result, undefined, skipQuantization);
   192→};
   193→

1→import { TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY } from './timeline-constants';
     2→
     3→const MINIMUM_TIMELINE_MAX = 30;
     4→
     5→export const getTimelineDimensions = (
     6→  framePositions: Map<string, number>,
     7→  pendingFrames?: (number | null)[]
     8→) => {
     9→  const positions = Array.from(framePositions.values());
    10→  const validPendingFrames = (pendingFrames || []).filter((f): f is number => f !== null && f !== undefined);
    11→  const allPositions = [...positions, ...validPendingFrames];
    12→
    13→  if (allPositions.length === 0) {
    14→    return { fullMin: 0, fullMax: MINIMUM_TIMELINE_MAX, fullRange: MINIMUM_TIMELINE_MAX };
    15→  }
    16→
    17→  const staticMax = Math.max(...allPositions, 0);
    18→  const staticMin = Math.min(...allPositions, 0);
    19→  const fullMax = Math.max(staticMax, MINIMUM_TIMELINE_MAX);
    20→  const fullMin = Math.min(0, staticMin);
    21→  const fullRange = Math.max(fullMax - fullMin, 1);
    22→
    23→  return { fullMin, fullMax, fullRange };
    24→};
    25→
    26→interface TrailingEffectiveEndInput {
    27→  framePositions: Map<string, number>;
    28→  imagesCount: number;
    29→  hasExistingTrailingVideo: boolean;
    30→  singleImageOffset?: number;
    31→  multiImageOffset?: number;
    32→}
    33→
    34→export function getTrailingEffectiveEnd({
    35→  framePositions,
    36→  imagesCount,
    37→  hasExistingTrailingVideo,
    38→  singleImageOffset = 49,
    39→  multiImageOffset = 17,
    40→}: TrailingEffectiveEndInput): number | null {
    41→  if (imagesCount === 0) {
    42→    return null;
    43→  }
    44→
    45→  const isMultiImage = imagesCount > 1;
    46→  if (isMultiImage && !hasExistingTrailingVideo) {
    47→    return null;
    48→  }
    49→
    50→  const trailingDefaultOffset = isMultiImage ? multiImageOffset : singleImageOffset;
    51→  const imageValues = [...framePositions.entries()]
    52→    .filter(([id]) => id !== TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY)
    53→    .map(([, value]) => value);
    54→  if (imageValues.length === 0) {
    55→    return null;
    56→  }
    57→  return Math.max(...imageValues) + trailingDefaultOffset;
    58→}
    59→

     3→// Get pair information from positions (excludes trailing endpoint)
     4→export const getPairInfo = (framePositions: Map<string, number>) => {
     5→  const sortedPositions = [...framePositions.entries()]
     6→    .filter(([id]) => id !== TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY)
     7→    .map(([id, pos]) => ({ id, pos }))
     8→    .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
     9→
    10→  const pairs = [];
    11→  for (let i = 0; i < sortedPositions.length - 1; i++) {
    12→    const startFrame = sortedPositions[i].pos;
    13→    const endFrame = sortedPositions[i + 1].pos;
    14→    const pairFrames = endFrame - startFrame;
    15→
    16→    pairs.push({
    17→      index: i,
    18→      startId: sortedPositions[i].id,
    19→      endId: sortedPositions[i + 1].id,
    20→      startFrame,
    21→      endFrame,
    22→      frames: pairFrames,
    23→      generationStart: startFrame,
    24→      contextStart: endFrame,
    25→      contextEnd: endFrame,
    26→    });
    27→  }
    28→
    29→  return pairs;
    30→};

1→/**
     2→ * Calculate placement for a new structure video on the timeline.
     3→ * Places after the last existing video, clipping the last video if needed to make space.
     4→ *
     5→ * @returns Object with start_frame, end_frame, and optional lastVideoUpdate if clipping is needed
     6→ */
     7→interface VideoPlacementResult {
     8→  start_frame: number;
     9→  end_frame: number;
    10→  lastVideoUpdate?: { index: number; newEndFrame: number };
    11→}
    12→
    13→export const calculateNewVideoPlacement = (
    14→  videoFrameCount: number,
    15→  existingVideos: Array<{ path: string; start_frame: number; end_frame: number }> | undefined,
    16→  fullMax: number
    17→): VideoPlacementResult => {
    18→  let start_frame = 0;
    19→  let end_frame = videoFrameCount;
    20→  let lastVideoUpdate: { index: number; newEndFrame: number } | undefined;
    21→
    22→  if (existingVideos && existingVideos.length > 0) {
    23→    const sorted = [...existingVideos].sort((a, b) => a.start_frame - b.start_frame);
    24→    const lastVideo = sorted[sorted.length - 1];
    25→    const lastVideoIndex = existingVideos.findIndex(
    26→      v => v.path === lastVideo.path && v.start_frame === lastVideo.start_frame
    27→    );
    28→
    29→    start_frame = lastVideo.end_frame;
    30→    end_frame = start_frame + videoFrameCount;
    31→
    32→    // If no space on timeline, clip 1/5 of the last video's range
    33→    if (start_frame >= fullMax && lastVideoIndex >= 0) {
    34→      const lastVideoRange = lastVideo.end_frame - lastVideo.start_frame;
    35→      const clipAmount = Math.max(10, Math.floor(lastVideoRange / 5));
    36→      const newLastVideoEnd = lastVideo.end_frame - clipAmount;
    37→
    38→      if (newLastVideoEnd > lastVideo.start_frame + 10) {
    39→        lastVideoUpdate = { index: lastVideoIndex, newEndFrame: newLastVideoEnd };
    40→        start_frame = newLastVideoEnd;
    41→        end_frame = start_frame + videoFrameCount;
    42→      }
    43→    }
    44→  }
    45→
    46→  return { start_frame, end_frame, lastVideoUpdate };
    47→};
    48→
    49→/**
    50→ * Extract pair_shot_generation_id from a generation row.
    51→ * Checks the FK column first, then falls back to params for legacy data.
    52→ */
    53→const getPairShotGenerationId = (
    54→  generation: {
    55→    pair_shot_generation_id?: string | null;
    56→    params?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    57→  }
    58→): string | null => {
    59→  // Check FK column first (new format)
    60→  if (generation.pair_shot_generation_id) {
    61→    return generation.pair_shot_generation_id;
    62→  }
    63→
    64→  // Fallback to params (legacy format)
    65→  const params = generation.params;
    66→  if (!params) return null;
    67→
    68→  const individualParams = params.individual_segment_params as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    69→  return (
    70→    (individualParams?.pair_shot_generation_id as string) ||
    71→    (params.pair_shot_generation_id as string) ||
    72→    null
    73→  );
    74→};
    75→
    76→/**
    77→ * Find trailing video info from a list of video outputs.
    78→ * A trailing video is one where pair_shot_generation_id matches the last image's shot_generation_id.
    79→ *
    80→ * @param videoOutputs - Array of generation rows (video outputs)
    81→ * @param lastImageShotGenId - The shot_generation_id of the last image on the timeline
    82→ * @returns Object with hasTrailing boolean and optional videoUrl
    83→ */
    84→export const findTrailingVideoInfo = (
    85→  videoOutputs: Array<{
    86→    type?: string | null;
    87→    location?: string | null;
    88→    pair_shot_generation_id?: string | null;
    89→    params?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    90→  }>,
    91→  lastImageShotGenId: string | null
    92→): { hasTrailing: boolean; videoUrl: string | null } => {
    93→  if (!videoOutputs || videoOutputs.length === 0 || !lastImageShotGenId) {
    94→    return { hasTrailing: false, videoUrl: null };
    95→  }
    96→
    97→  const trailingVideo = videoOutputs.find(gen => {
    98→    // Must be a video with a location (completed)
    99→    if (!gen.type?.includes('video') || !gen.location) return false;
   100→    // Check if pair_shot_generation_id matches the last image
   101→    const pairId = getPairShotGenerationId(gen);
   102→    return pairId === lastImageShotGenId;
   103→  });
   104→
   105→  return {
   106→    hasTrailing: !!trailingVideo,
   107→    videoUrl: trailingVideo?.location || null,
   108→  };
   109→};
   110→
```

1→import {
     2→  createTimelineWriteQueueLogger,
     3→  type TimelineWriteEventHandler,
     4→} from '@/shared/lib/timelineWriteLogging';
     5→import {
     6→  isTimelineWriteTimeoutError,
     7→  runTimelineWriteWithTimeout,
     8→} from '@/shared/lib/timelineWriteTimeout';
     9→
    10→export {
    11→  createTimelineWriteQueueLogger,
    12→  type TimelineWriteEventHandler,
    13→} from '@/shared/lib/timelineWriteLogging';
    14→export {
    15→  isTimelineWriteTimeoutError,
    16→  runTimelineWriteWithTimeout,
    17→} from '@/shared/lib/timelineWriteTimeout';
    18→
    19→const shotWriteTail = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
    20→const shotWriteDepth = new Map<string, number>();
    21→const shotWriteActive = new Map<string, { requestId: number; operation: string; startedAt: number }>();
    22→let writeRequestCounter = 0;
    23→const DEFAULT_SERIALIZED_TIMELINE_TASK_TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;
    24→
    25→interface TimelineSerializedWriteOptions {
    26→  timeoutMs?: number;
    27→  onTimeout?: (meta: {
    28→    shotId: string;
    29→    operation: string;
    30→    timeoutMs: number;
    31→    pendingMs: number;
    32→    requestId: number;
    33→  }) => void;
    34→}
    35→
    36→/**
    37→ * Returns true if any serialized timeline write is active or queued for the
    38→ * given shot. Both the timeline drag path and the batch editor path go through
    39→ * runSerializedTimelineWrite, so this covers all writes across both paths.
    40→ * Used by useTimelinePositions.syncFromDatabase to suppress stale syncs while
    41→ * the batch editor is writing (the batch editor does not set isLockedRef, so
    42→ * without this check a background refetch during the midpoint RPC would snap
    43→ * positions back to pre-edit values).
    44→ */
    45→export function isTimelineWriteActive(shotId: string): boolean {
    46→  return (shotWriteDepth.get(shotId) ?? 0) > 0;
    47→}
    48→
    49→/**
    50→ * Serialize timeline writes per shot to avoid overlapping updates from
    51→ * different UI flows (batch reorder, timeline drag, etc.).
    52→ */
    53→export async function runSerializedTimelineWrite<T>(
    54→  shotId: string,
    55→  operation: string,
    56→  task: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T>,
    57→  onEvent?: TimelineWriteEventHandler,
    58→  options?: TimelineSerializedWriteOptions,
    59→): Promise<T> {
    60→  const requestId = ++writeRequestCounter;
    61→  const queuedAt = Date.now();
    62→  const previousTail = shotWriteTail.get(shotId) ?? Promise.resolve();
    63→  const nextDepth = (shotWriteDepth.get(shotId) ?? 0) + 1;
    64→  shotWriteDepth.set(shotId, nextDepth);
    65→  const blockedBy = shotWriteActive.get(shotId);
    66→  const blockedByDurationMs = blockedBy ? Math.max(0, queuedAt - blockedBy.startedAt) : null;
    67→
    68→  onEvent?.('queued', {
    69→    shotId,
    70→    operation,
    71→    waitMs: 0,
    72→    durationMs: 0,
    73→    queueDepth: Math.max(0, nextDepth - 1),
    74→    requestId,
    75→    blockedByOperation: blockedBy?.operation ?? null,
    76→    blockedByDurationMs,
    77→  });
    78→
    79→  let releaseTail!: () => void;
    80→  const currentTail = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
    81→    releaseTail = resolve;
    82→  });
    83→
    84→  const chainedTail = previousTail.catch(() => undefined).then(() => currentTail);
    85→  shotWriteTail.set(shotId, chainedTail);
    86→
    87→  await previousTail.catch(() => undefined);
    88→
    89→  const startedAt = Date.now();
    90→  shotWriteActive.set(shotId, {
    91→    requestId,
    92→    operation,
    93→    startedAt,
    94→  });
    95→  onEvent?.('start', {
    96→    shotId,
    97→    operation,
    98→    waitMs: startedAt - queuedAt,
    99→    durationMs: 0,
   100→    queueDepth: Math.max(0, (shotWriteDepth.get(shotId) ?? 1) - 1),
   101→    requestId,
   102→    blockedByOperation: blockedBy?.operation ?? null,
   103→    blockedByDurationMs,
   104→  });
   105→
   106→  try {
   107→    const timeoutMs = options?.timeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_SERIALIZED_TIMELINE_TASK_TIMEOUT_MS;
   108→    return await runTimelineWriteWithTimeout(
   109→      operation,
   110→      task,
   111→      {
   112→        timeoutMs,
   113→        onTimeout: ({ pendingMs }) => {
   114→          options?.onTimeout?.({
   115→            shotId,
   116→            operation,
   117→            timeoutMs,
   118→            pendingMs,
   119→            requestId,
   120→          });
   121→        },
   122→      },
   123→    );
   124→  } finally {
   125→    releaseTail();
   126→
   127→    const remainingDepth = Math.max(0, (shotWriteDepth.get(shotId) ?? 1) - 1);
   128→    if (remainingDepth === 0) {
   129→      shotWriteDepth.delete(shotId);
   130→    } else {
   131→      shotWriteDepth.set(shotId, remainingDepth);
   132→    }
   133→
   134→    if (shotWriteTail.get(shotId) === chainedTail) {
   135→      shotWriteTail.delete(shotId);
   136→    }
   137→    if (shotWriteActive.get(shotId)?.requestId === requestId) {
   138→      shotWriteActive.delete(shotId);
   139→    }
   140→
   141→    onEvent?.('end', {
   142→      shotId,
   143→      operation,
   144→      waitMs: startedAt - queuedAt,
   145→      durationMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
   146→      queueDepth: Math.max(0, remainingDepth - 1),
   147→      requestId,
   148→      blockedByOperation: blockedBy?.operation ?? null,
   149→      blockedByDurationMs,
   150→    });
   151→  }
   152→}
   153→

1→type TimelineWritePhase = 'queued' | 'start' | 'end';
     2→
     3→interface TimelineWriteEventMeta {
     4→  shotId: string;
     5→  operation: string;
     6→  waitMs: number;
     7→  durationMs: number;
     8→  queueDepth: number;
     9→  requestId: number;
    10→  blockedByOperation?: string | null;
    11→  blockedByDurationMs?: number | null;
    12→}
    13→
    14→export type TimelineWriteEventHandler = (phase: TimelineWritePhase, meta: TimelineWriteEventMeta) => void;
    15→
    16→interface TimelineWriteLoggerOptions {
    17→  logPrefix: string;
    18→  log?: (...args: Parameters<typeof console.log>) => void;
    19→}
    20→
    21→function shortTimelineId(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
    22→  return value ? value.slice(0, 8) : null;
    23→}
    24→
    25→/**
    26→ * Shared queue-phase logger for timeline write serialization.
    27→ * Keeps queue observability structure consistent across timeline hooks.
    28→ */
    29→export function createTimelineWriteQueueLogger(options: TimelineWriteLoggerOptions): TimelineWriteEventHandler {
    30→  const log = options.log ?? console.log;
    31→  return (phase, meta) => {
    32→    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
    33→      shotId: shortTimelineId(meta.shotId),
    34→      requestId: meta.requestId,
    35→      operation: meta.operation,
    36→      waitMs: meta.waitMs,
    37→      queueDepth: meta.queueDepth,
    38→      blockedByOperation: meta.blockedByOperation ?? null,
    39→      blockedByDurationMs: meta.blockedByDurationMs ?? null,
    40→    };
    41→
    42→    if (phase !== 'start') {
    43→      payload.durationMs = meta.durationMs;
    44→    }
    45→
    46→    log(`${options.logPrefix} write queue ${phase}`, payload);
    47→  };
    48→}
    49→

1→import { isAbortError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/errorUtils';
     2→
     3→const DEFAULT_TIMELINE_WRITE_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000;
     4→const TIMELINE_WRITE_TIMEOUT_CODE = 'TIMELINE_WRITE_TIMEOUT';
     5→
     6→interface TimelineWriteTimeoutOptions {
     7→  timeoutMs?: number;
     8→  onTimeout?: (meta: { operation: string; timeoutMs: number; pendingMs: number }) => void;
     9→  upstreamSignal?: AbortSignal;
    10→}
    11→
    12→class TimelineWriteTimeoutError extends Error {
    13→  readonly code = TIMELINE_WRITE_TIMEOUT_CODE;
    14→
    15→  constructor(operation: string, timeoutMs: number, cause?: unknown) {
    16→    super(`Timeline write "${operation}" timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`);
    17→    this.name = 'TimelineWriteTimeoutError';
    18→    if (cause !== undefined) {
    19→      (this as { cause?: unknown }).cause = cause;
    20→    }
    21→  }
    22→}
    23→
    24→export function isTimelineWriteTimeoutError(error: unknown): error is TimelineWriteTimeoutError {
    25→  return (
    26→    error instanceof TimelineWriteTimeoutError
    27→    || (
    28→      typeof error === 'object'
    29→      && error !== null
    30→      && 'code' in error
    31→      && (error as { code?: unknown }).code === TIMELINE_WRITE_TIMEOUT_CODE
    32→    )
    33→  );
    34→}
    35→
    36→/**
    37→ * Guard a write request with an abortable timeout so stalled network calls
    38→ * cannot block the serialized per-shot queue forever.
    39→ */
    40→export async function runTimelineWriteWithTimeout<T>(
    41→  operation: string,
    42→  task: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T>,
    43→  options?: TimelineWriteTimeoutOptions,
    44→): Promise<T> {
    45→  const timeoutMs = options?.timeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_TIMELINE_WRITE_TIMEOUT_MS;
    46→  const startedAt = Date.now();
    47→  const controller = new AbortController();
    48→  let timedOut = false;
    49→  let abortedByUpstream = false;
    50→
    51→  const upstreamSignal = options?.upstreamSignal;
    52→  const handleUpstreamAbort = () => {
    53→    abortedByUpstream = true;
    54→    controller.abort();
    55→  };
    56→  if (upstreamSignal) {
    57→    if (upstreamSignal.aborted) {
    58→      handleUpstreamAbort();
    59→    } else {
    60→      upstreamSignal.addEventListener('abort', handleUpstreamAbort, { once: true });
    61→    }
    62→  }
    63→
    64→  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
    65→    timedOut = true;
    66→    options?.onTimeout?.({
    67→      operation,
    68→      timeoutMs,
    69→      pendingMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
    70→    });
    71→    controller.abort();
    72→  }, timeoutMs);
    73→
    74→  try {
    75→    return await task(controller.signal);
    76→  } catch (error) {
    77→    if (timedOut || (abortedByUpstream && controller.signal.aborted) || (controller.signal.aborted && isAbortError(error))) {
    78→      throw new TimelineWriteTimeoutError(operation, timeoutMs, error);
    79→    }
    80→    throw error;
    81→  } finally {
    82→    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    83→    if (upstreamSignal) {
    84→      upstreamSignal.removeEventListener('abort', handleUpstreamAbort);
    85→    }
    86→  }
    87→}
    88→
```

tool_result Read
```
1→/**
     2→ * Utility for extracting poster images (first frame) from video files
     3→ */
     4→
     5→/**
     6→ * Extract the first frame of a video as a Blob
     7→ * @param videoFile The video file to extract from
     8→ * @returns Promise that resolves to a Blob of the poster image (JPEG)
     9→ */
    10→export function extractVideoPosterFrame(videoFile: File): Promise<Blob> {
    11→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    12→    const video = document.createElement('video');
    13→    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    14→    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    15→
    16→    if (!ctx) {
    17→      reject(new Error('Failed to get canvas context'));
    18→      return;
    19→    }
    20→
    21→    video.preload = 'metadata';
    22→    video.muted = true;
    23→    video.playsInline = true;
    24→
    25→    // When metadata is loaded, we can get dimensions
    26→    video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', () => {
    27→      canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
    28→      canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
    29→      
    30→      // Seek to a small time to avoid black frames at the start
    31→      video.currentTime = 0.1;
    32→    });
    33→
    34→    // When we've seeked to the right time, capture the frame
    35→    video.addEventListener('seeked', () => {
    36→      try {
    37→        // Draw the video frame to canvas
    38→        ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    39→        
    40→        // Convert canvas to blob
    41→        canvas.toBlob(
    42→          (blob) => {
    43→            if (blob) {
    44→              resolve(blob);
    45→            } else {
    46→              reject(new Error('Failed to create blob from canvas'));
    47→            }
    48→            
    49→            // Cleanup
    50→            video.src = '';
    51→            URL.revokeObjectURL(video.src);
    52→          },
    53→          'image/jpeg',
    54→          0.85 // Quality
    55→        );
    56→      } catch (error) {
    57→        reject(error);
    58→      }
    59→    });
    60→
    61→    video.addEventListener('error', () => {
    62→      reject(new Error('Failed to load video'));
    63→    });
    64→
    65→    // Load the video
    66→    video.src = URL.createObjectURL(videoFile);
    67→  });
    68→}
    69→
    70→/**
    71→ * Extract the final frame of a video as a Blob
    72→ * @param videoFile The video file to extract from
    73→ * @returns Promise that resolves to a Blob of the final frame image (JPEG)
    74→ */
    75→export function extractVideoFinalFrame(videoFile: File): Promise<Blob> {
    76→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    77→    const video = document.createElement('video');
    78→    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    79→    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    80→
    81→    if (!ctx) {
    82→      reject(new Error('Failed to get canvas context'));
    83→      return;
    84→    }
    85→
    86→    video.preload = 'metadata';
    87→    video.muted = true;
    88→    video.playsInline = true;
    89→
    90→    // When metadata is loaded, we can get dimensions and duration
    91→    video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', () => {
    92→      canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
    93→      canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
    94→      
    95→      // Seek to near the end (0.1 seconds before) to avoid potential issues
    96→      video.currentTime = Math.max(0, video.duration - 0.1);
    97→    });
    98→
    99→    // When we've seeked to the right time, capture the frame
   100→    video.addEventListener('seeked', () => {
   101→      try {
   102→        // Draw the video frame to canvas
   103→        ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
   104→        
   105→        // Convert canvas to blob
   106→        canvas.toBlob(
   107→          (blob) => {
   108→            if (blob) {
   109→              resolve(blob);
   110→            } else {
   111→              reject(new Error('Failed to create blob from canvas'));
   112→            }
   113→            
   114→            // Cleanup
   115→            video.src = '';
   116→            URL.revokeObjectURL(video.src);
   117→          },
   118→          'image/jpeg',
   119→          0.85 // Quality
   120→        );
   121→      } catch (error) {
   122→        reject(error);
   123→      }
   124→    });
   125→
   126→    video.addEventListener('error', () => {
   127→      reject(new Error('Failed to load video'));
   128→    });
   129→
   130→    // Load the video
   131→    video.src = URL.createObjectURL(videoFile);
   132→  });
   133→}
   134→
