1→/**
     2→ * Video Thumbnail Generator
     3→ *
     4→ * Extracts thumbnails from videos on the client side and uploads them to storage.
     5→ * This is used to generate missing thumbnails for videos that don't have them.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     9→import { getDisplayUrl } from '@/shared/lib/media/mediaUrl';
    10→import { storagePaths, MEDIA_BUCKET } from '@/shared/lib/storagePaths';
    11→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
    12→
    13→interface ThumbnailGenerationResult {
    14→  success: boolean;
    15→  thumbnailUrl?: string;
    16→  error?: string;
    17→}
    18→
    19→interface ThumbnailExtractResult {
    20→  success: boolean;
    21→  thumbnailUrl?: string;
    22→  error?: string;
    23→}
    24→
    25→/**
    26→ * Extracts a thumbnail from a video at a specific time
    27→ */
    28→function extractThumbnailFromVideo(
    29→  videoUrl: string,
    30→  timeInSeconds: number = 0.001
    31→): Promise<Blob> {
    32→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    33→    const video = document.createElement('video');
    34→    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    35→    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    36→
    37→    if (!ctx) {
    38→      reject(new Error('Failed to get canvas context'));
    39→      return;
    40→    }
    41→
    42→    video.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    43→    video.preload = 'metadata';
    44→    video.muted = true;
    45→    video.playsInline = true;
    46→
    47→    // Handle video loaded
    48→    video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', () => {
    49→      // Set canvas size to video dimensions (or max 1280px width)
    50→      const maxWidth = 1280;
    51→      const scale = Math.min(1, maxWidth / video.videoWidth);
    52→      canvas.width = video.videoWidth * scale;
    53→      canvas.height = video.videoHeight * scale;
    54→
    55→      // Seek to the desired time (or start if video is shorter)
    56→      video.currentTime = Math.min(timeInSeconds, video.duration - 0.1);
    57→    });
    58→
    59→    // Handle seeked event
    60→    video.addEventListener('seeked', () => {
    61→      try {
    62→        // Draw video frame to canvas
    63→        ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    64→
    65→        // Convert to blob (JPEG, 85% quality)
    66→        canvas.toBlob(
    67→          (blob) => {
    68→            video.remove();
    69→            if (blob) {
    70→              resolve(blob);
    71→            } else {
    72→              reject(new Error('Failed to create blob from canvas'));
    73→            }
    74→          },
    75→          'image/jpeg',
    76→          0.85
    77→        );
    78→      } catch (error) {
    79→        video.remove();
    80→        reject(error);
    81→      }
    82→    });
    83→
    84→    // Handle errors
    85→    video.addEventListener('error', () => {
    86→      video.remove();
    87→      reject(new Error(`Video loading failed: ${video.error?.message || 'Unknown error'}`));
    88→    });
    89→
    90→    // Start loading
    91→    video.src = getDisplayUrl(videoUrl);
    92→  });
    93→}
    94→
    95→/**
    96→ * Uploads a thumbnail blob to Supabase storage
    97→ * Note: projectId param kept for backwards compatibility but no longer used in path
    98→ */
    99→async function uploadThumbnailToStorage(
   100→  blob: Blob,
   101→  generationId: string,
   102→  _projectId: string
   103→): Promise<string> {
   104→  // Get userId for storage path
   105→  const { data: { session } } = await supabase().auth.getSession();
   106→  if (!session?.user?.id) {
   107→    throw new Error('User not authenticated');
   108→  }
   109→  const userId = session.user.id;
   110→
   111→  const fileName = `${generationId}-thumb.jpg`;
   112→  const filePath = storagePaths.thumbnail(userId, fileName);
   113→
   114→  const { error } = await supabase().storage
   115→    .from(MEDIA_BUCKET)
   116→    .upload(filePath, blob, {
   117→      contentType: 'image/jpeg',
   118→      upsert: true, // Overwrite if exists
   119→    });
   120→
   121→  if (error) {
   122→    console.error('[ThumbnailGenerator] Upload failed:', error);
   123→    throw error;
   124→  }
   125→
   126→  // Get public URL
   127→  const { data: urlData } = supabase().storage
   128→    .from(MEDIA_BUCKET)
   129→    .getPublicUrl(filePath);
   130→
   131→  return urlData.publicUrl;
   132→}
   133→
   134→/**
   135→ * Updates the generation record with the new thumbnail URL
   136→ */
   137→async function updateGenerationThumbnail(
   138→  generationId: string,
   139→  thumbnailUrl: string
   140→): Promise<void> {
   141→
   142→  const { error } = await supabase().from('generations')
   143→    .update({ thumbnail_url: thumbnailUrl })
   144→    .eq('id', generationId);
   145→
   146→  if (error) {
   147→    console.error('[ThumbnailGenerator] Database update failed:', error);
   148→    throw error;
   149→  }
   150→}
   151→
   152→/**
   153→ * Main function: Generates and uploads a thumbnail for a video
   154→ */
   155→export async function generateAndUploadThumbnail(
   156→  videoUrl: string,
   157→  generationId: string,
   158→  projectId: string
   159→): Promise<ThumbnailGenerationResult> {
   160→  try {
   161→
   162→    // Step 1: Extract thumbnail from video (first frame)
   163→    const thumbnailBlob = await extractThumbnailFromVideo(videoUrl, 0.001);
   164→
   165→    // Step 2: Upload to storage
   166→    const thumbnailUrl = await uploadThumbnailToStorage(thumbnailBlob, generationId, projectId);
   167→
   168→    // Step 3: Update database
   169→    await updateGenerationThumbnail(generationId, thumbnailUrl);
   170→
   171→    return {
   172→      success: true,
   173→      thumbnailUrl,
   174→    };
   175→  } catch (error) {
   176→    normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'VideoThumbnailGenerator', showToast: false });
   177→
   178→    return {
   179→      success: false,
   180→      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
   181→    };
   182→  }
   183→}
   184→
   185→/**
   186→ * Extract and upload thumbnail only (no database update)
   187→ * Use this for variants where you'll update the record yourself
   188→ */
   189→export async function extractAndUploadThumbnailOnly(
   190→  videoUrl: string,
   191→  uniqueId: string,
   192→  projectId: string
   193→): Promise<ThumbnailExtractResult> {
   194→  try {
   195→
   196→    // Step 1: Extract thumbnail from video (first frame)
   197→    const thumbnailBlob = await extractThumbnailFromVideo(videoUrl, 0.001);
   198→    
   199→    // Step 2: Upload to storage
   200→    const thumbnailUrl = await uploadThumbnailToStorage(thumbnailBlob, uniqueId, projectId);
   201→    
   202→    return {
   203→      success: true,
   204→      thumbnailUrl,
   205→    };
   206→  } catch (error) {
   207→    normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'VideoThumbnailGenerator', showToast: false });
   208→
   209→    return {
   210→      success: false,
   211→      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
   212→    };
   213→  }
   214→}
   215→

1→/** Shared video/frame utility helpers. */
     2→
     3→const FPS = 16;
     4→
     5→// Wan models require frame counts in the form 4N + 1.
     6→
     7→export function isValidFrameCount(frames: number): boolean {
     8→  return frames > 0 && (frames - 1) % 4 === 0;
     9→}
    10→
    11→export function quantizeFrameCount(frames: number, minFrames: number = 1): number {
    12→  const validMin = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((minFrames - 1) / 4) * 4 + 1);
    13→
    14→  if (frames <= validMin) return validMin;
    15→
    16→  const quantizationFactor = Math.round((frames - 1) / 4);
    17→  const quantized = quantizationFactor * 4 + 1;
    18→
    19→  return Math.max(validMin, quantized);
    20→}
    21→
    22→
    23→export function getValidFrameCounts(minFrames: number, maxFrames: number): number[] {
    24→  const counts: number[] = [];
    25→  let current = Math.ceil((minFrames - 1) / 4) * 4 + 1;
    26→  if (current < minFrames) current += 4;
    27→
    28→  while (current <= maxFrames) {
    29→    counts.push(current);
    30→    current += 4;
    31→  }
    32→  return counts;
    33→}
    34→
    35→export function quantizeGap(gap: number, minGap: number = 5): number {
    36→  return quantizeFrameCount(Math.max(gap, minGap), minGap);
    37→}
    38→
    39→export function framesToSeconds(frame: number): string {
    40→  const seconds = frame / FPS;
    41→  return `${seconds.toFixed(2)}s`;
    42→}
    43→
    44→export function framesToSecondsValue(frame: number): number {
    45→  return frame / FPS;
    46→}
    47→

1→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
     2→
     3→export interface VideoMetadata {
     4→  duration_seconds: number;
     5→  frame_rate: number;
     6→  total_frames: number;
     7→  width: number;
     8→  height: number;
     9→  file_size: number;
    10→}
    11→
    12→/**
    13→ * Extracts video metadata using HTML5 Video API
    14→ */
    15→export const extractVideoMetadata = (file: File): Promise<VideoMetadata> => {
    16→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    17→    const video = document.createElement('video');
    18→    video.preload = 'metadata';
    19→
    20→    video.onloadedmetadata = () => {
    21→      const duration = video.duration;
    22→      const width = video.videoWidth;
    23→      const height = video.videoHeight;
    24→
    25→      // Estimate frame rate (assume 30fps as standard, could be improved)
    26→      const frameRate = 30;
    27→      const totalFrames = Math.floor(duration * frameRate);
    28→
    29→      URL.revokeObjectURL(video.src);
    30→
    31→      resolve({
    32→        duration_seconds: duration,
    33→        frame_rate: frameRate,
    34→        total_frames: totalFrames,
    35→        width,
    36→        height,
    37→        file_size: file.size
    38→      });
    39→    };
    40→
    41→    video.onerror = () => {
    42→      URL.revokeObjectURL(video.src);
    43→      reject(new Error('Failed to load video metadata'));
    44→    };
    45→
    46→    video.src = URL.createObjectURL(file);
    47→  });
    48→};
    49→
    50→/**
    51→ * Extracts video metadata from a URL (for videos already uploaded)
    52→ */
    53→export const extractVideoMetadataFromUrl = (videoUrl: string): Promise<VideoMetadata> => {
    54→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    55→    const video = document.createElement('video');
    56→    video.preload = 'metadata';
    57→    video.crossOrigin = 'anonymous'; // Handle CORS for external URLs
    58→
    59→    video.onloadedmetadata = () => {
    60→      const duration = video.duration;
    61→      const width = video.videoWidth;
    62→      const height = video.videoHeight;
    63→
    64→      // Estimate frame rate (assume 30fps as standard, could be improved)
    65→      const frameRate = 30;
    66→      const totalFrames = Math.floor(duration * frameRate);
    67→
    68→      resolve({
    69→        duration_seconds: duration,
    70→        frame_rate: frameRate,
    71→        total_frames: totalFrames,
    72→        width,
    73→        height,
    74→        file_size: 0 // Unknown from URL
    75→      });
    76→    };
    77→
    78→    video.onerror = () => {
    79→      normalizeAndPresentError(new Error('Failed to load video metadata from URL'), { context: 'videoUploader:extractMetadata', showToast: false });
    80→      reject(new Error('Failed to load video metadata from URL'));
    81→    };
    82→
    83→    video.src = videoUrl;
    84→  });
    85→};
    86→

1→export function readEdgeErrorCode(payload: Record<string, unknown> | null): string | null {
     2→  if (!payload) {
     3→    return null;
     4→  }
     5→  if (typeof payload.errorCode === 'string' && payload.errorCode.trim().length > 0) {
     6→    return payload.errorCode;
     7→  }
     8→  if (typeof payload.error === 'string' && payload.error.trim().length > 0) {
     9→    return payload.error;
    10→  }
    11→  return null;
    12→}
    13→
```

1→import { type OperationResult, operationFailure, operationSuccess } from './edgeOperation.ts';
     2→import { SystemLogger } from './systemLogger.ts';
     3→
     4→function isJsonObject(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
     5→  return !!value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
     6→}
     7→
     8→function coerceLooseObject(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
     9→  return isJsonObject(value) ? value : {};
    10→}
    11→
    12→export interface ParseJsonBodyOptions {
    13→  mode?: 'strict' | 'loose';
    14→  message?: string;
    15→  nonObjectMessage?: string;
    16→}
    17→
    18→export async function parseJsonBody(
    19→  req: Request,
    20→  logger?: SystemLogger,
    21→  options?: ParseJsonBodyOptions,
    22→): Promise<OperationResult<Record<string, unknown>>> {
    23→  const mode = options?.mode ?? 'strict';
    24→  const message = options?.message ?? 'Invalid JSON body';
    25→  const nonObjectMessage = options?.nonObjectMessage ?? 'JSON body must be an object';
    26→
    27→  try {
    28→    const bodyText = await req.text();
    29→    if (!bodyText) {
    30→      return operationSuccess({}, { policy: 'best_effort' });
    31→    }
    32→
    33→    const parsed = JSON.parse(bodyText);
    34→    if (isJsonObject(parsed)) {
    35→      return operationSuccess(parsed, { policy: 'best_effort' });
    36→    }
    37→
    38→    if (mode === 'loose') {
    39→      logger?.debug('Non-object JSON body provided, using empty object defaults');
    40→      return operationSuccess(coerceLooseObject(parsed), { policy: 'fail_open' });
    41→    }
    42→
    43→    logger?.error(nonObjectMessage);
    44→    if (logger) {
    45→      await logger.flush();
    46→    }
    47→    return operationFailure(new Error(nonObjectMessage), {
    48→      policy: 'fail_closed',
    49→      errorCode: 'invalid_json_body_shape',
    50→      message: nonObjectMessage,
    51→      recoverable: false,
    52→      cause: parsed,
    53→    });
    54→  } catch (error) {
    55→    if (mode === 'loose') {
    56→      logger?.debug('No valid JSON body provided, using defaults');
    57→      return operationSuccess({}, { policy: 'fail_open' });
    58→    }
    59→    logger?.error(message);
    60→    if (logger) {
    61→      await logger.flush();
    62→    }
    63→    return operationFailure(error, {
    64→      policy: 'fail_closed',
    65→      errorCode: 'invalid_json_body',
    66→      message,
    67→      recoverable: false,
    68→      cause: error,
    69→    });
    70→  }
    71→}
    72→
    73→export async function parseJsonBodyStrict(
    74→  req: Request,
    75→  logger?: SystemLogger,
    76→  options?: ParseJsonBodyOptions,
    77→): Promise<OperationResult<Record<string, unknown>>> {
    78→  return parseJsonBody(req, logger, {
    79→    mode: 'strict',
    80→    message: options?.message,
    81→    nonObjectMessage: options?.nonObjectMessage,
    82→  });
    83→}
    84→
```

1→import { createClient } from './supabaseClient.ts';
     2→import { SystemLogger } from './systemLogger.ts';
     3→import {
     4→  type OperationResult,
     5→  operationFailure,
     6→  operationSuccess,
     7→} from './edgeOperation.ts';
     8→
     9→declare const Deno: { env: { get: (key: string) => string | undefined } };
    10→
    11→export interface EdgeRuntime {
    12→  supabaseAdmin: ReturnType<typeof createClient>;
    13→  logger: SystemLogger;
    14→}
    15→
    16→export interface InitEdgeRuntimeOptions {
    17→  clientOptions?: NonNullable<Parameters<typeof createClient>[2]>;
    18→}
    19→
    20→export function initEdgeRuntime(
    21→  functionName: string,
    22→  missingEnvLogPrefix: string,
    23→  options?: InitEdgeRuntimeOptions,
    24→): OperationResult<EdgeRuntime> {
    25→  const serviceKey = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY');
    26→  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL');
    27→
    28→  if (!serviceKey || !supabaseUrl) {
    29→    return operationFailure(new Error('Server configuration error'), {
    30→      policy: 'fail_closed',
    31→      errorCode: 'server_configuration_error',
    32→      message: 'Server configuration error',
    33→      recoverable: false,
    34→      cause: { missingEnvLogPrefix },
    35→    });
    36→  }
    37→
    38→  const supabaseAdmin = options?.clientOptions
    39→    ? createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey, options.clientOptions)
    40→    : createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey);
    41→  const logger = new SystemLogger(supabaseAdmin, functionName);
    42→
    43→  return operationSuccess({ supabaseAdmin, logger }, { policy: 'best_effort' });
    44→}
    45→
    46→export async function ensureRequestMethod(
    47→  req: Request,
    48→  method: string,
    49→  logger?: SystemLogger,
    50→): Promise<OperationResult<void>> {
    51→  if (req.method === method) {
    52→    return operationSuccess(undefined, { policy: 'best_effort' });
    53→  }
    54→
    55→  logger?.warn('Method not allowed', { method: req.method });
    56→  if (logger) {
    57→    await logger.flush();
    58→  }
    59→  return operationFailure(new Error('Method not allowed'), {
    60→    policy: 'fail_closed',
    61→    errorCode: 'method_not_allowed',
    62→    message: 'Method not allowed',
    63→    recoverable: false,
    64→    cause: { method: req.method, allow: method },
    65→  });
    66→}
    67→

Excellent. Let me create a comprehensive summary of my findings:

## Assessment: useSharedLightboxState Decomposition

### Finding: **This split adds file-jumping overhead without benefit and should be undone.**

### Evidence:

**1. Sub-hooks are ONLY consumed by the parent hook**
   - `useSharedLightboxInteractionState` is imported only in `useSharedLightboxState.ts` (line 11)
   - `useLightboxPanelModel` (renamed from `useSharedLightboxPresentationState`) is imported only in `useSharedLightboxState.ts` (line 12)
   - No other files in the codebase import these sub-hooks directly
   - `useSharedLightboxState.types.ts` is imported by all three files (the parent + both sub-hooks), but types should be co-located for this level of cohesion

**2. The decomposition creates unnecessary coupling**
   - The parent hook (`useSharedLightboxState.ts`, lines 97-116) is a thin orchestrator that just calls these sub-hooks in sequence and reassembles their outputs
   - `useSharedLightboxInteractionState` depends on `VariantsStateResult` from the parent's variants state
   - `useLightboxPanelModel` depends on both `VariantsStateResult` and `InteractionState` from the previous hook
   - This creates a dependency chain that requires reading 4 files to understand the full flow: types → variants → interaction → presentation → main

**3. The split hasn't reduced complexity meaningfully**
   - Combined size: 757 lines across 4 files
   - The parent hook is 24 lines of orchestration logic with zero actual business logic
   - Line-jumping required: `useSharedLightboxState.ts` → line 11-12 imports → `useSharedLightboxInteractionState.ts` (140+ lines) → back to types
   - Every change to the state shape requires updates across multiple files

**4. Anti-pattern match**
   - Per CLAUDE.md: "**Don't over-abstract** - shared functions with many conditionals add complexity, two similar functions can be clearer"
   - Per CLAUDE.md: "**Prefer existing patterns** - Reuse shared components/hooks in `src/shared/` before creating new ones"
   - This split violates the principle of keeping closely-coupled logic in one place
   - No evidence of the sub-hooks being consumed independently (which would justify the split)

### Recommendation:

Inline `useSharedLightboxInteractionState` and `useLightboxPanelModel` back into `useSharedLightboxState.ts`. Keep types in a separate `useSharedLightboxState.types.ts` for readability, but merge the logic into a single implementation file. This:
- Reduces file-jumping from 4 files to 2 (main + types)
- Makes the orchestration logic visible in one place
- Preserves modularity within the file through helper functions (`useSharedMakeMainVariantState`, `useLightboxShotActions`, etc.) which are already internal
- Aligns with the "Fix > wontfix" principle — this is a fix that makes the codebase smaller and clearer

    53→export interface EdgeRequestContext<TBody extends Record<string, unknown>> {
    54→  req: Request;
    55→  body: TBody;
    56→  supabaseAdmin: EdgeRuntime['supabaseAdmin'];
    57→  logger: EdgeRuntime['logger'];
    58→  auth: AuthResult | null;
    59→}
    60→
    61→type EdgeBootstrapResult<TBody extends Record<string, unknown>> =
    62→  | { ok: true; value: EdgeRequestContext<TBody> }
    63→  | { ok: false; response: Response };
    64→
    65→function deriveErrorCode(message: string, status: number): string {
    66→  const normalized = message.trim().toLowerCase();
    67→  if (normalized === 'method not allowed' || status === 405) {
    68→    return 'method_not_allowed';
    69→  }
    70→  if (normalized === 'authentication failed' || status === 401) {
    71→    return 'authentication_failed';
    72→  }
    73→  if (
    74→    normalized === 'unauthorized - service role required'
    75→    || (normalized.includes('unauthorized') && normalized.includes('service role'))
    76→  ) {
    77→    return 'service_role_required';
    78→  }
    79→  if (normalized === 'internal server error' || status >= 500) {
    80→    return 'internal_server_error';
    81→  }
    82→  if (status === 403) {
    83→    return 'forbidden';
    84→  }
    85→  return 'request_failed';
    86→}
    87→
    88→function isRecoverableStatus(status: number): boolean {
    89→  return status >= 500 || status === 429;
    90→}
    91→
    92→function buildErrorResponse(
    93→  message: string,
    94→  status: number,
    95→  format: ErrorResponseFormat,
    96→  extraHeaders?: Record<string, string>,
    97→): Response {
    98→  if (format === 'text') {
    99→    return new Response(message, {
   100→      status,
   101→      headers: extraHeaders,
   102→    });
   103→  }
   104→  return edgeErrorResponse(
   105→    {
   106→      errorCode: deriveErrorCode(message, status),
   107→      message,
   108→      recoverable: isRecoverableStatus(status),
   109→    },
   110→    status,
   111→    extraHeaders,
   112→  );
   113→}
   114→
   115→function parseMode(options: EdgeBootstrapOptions): BodyParseMode {
   116→  return options.parseBody ?? 'none';
   117→}
   118→
   119→function methodFor(options: EdgeBootstrapOptions): string {
   120→  return options.method ?? 'POST';
   121→}
   122→
   123→function authIsRequired(options: EdgeBootstrapOptions): boolean {
   124→  return options.auth?.required ?? true;
   125→}
   126→
   127→function errorFormat(options: EdgeBootstrapOptions): ErrorResponseFormat {
   128→  return options.errorResponseFormat ?? 'json';
   129→}
   130→
   131→/**
   132→ * Shared bootstrap for edge handlers:

   134→ * - method guard
   135→ * - runtime initialization
   136→ * - optional JSON body parsing
   137→ * - optional authentication + service-role policy
   138→ */
   139→export async function bootstrapEdgeHandler<TBody extends Record<string, unknown> = Record<string, unknown>>(
   140→  req: Request,
   141→  options: EdgeBootstrapOptions,
   142→): Promise<EdgeBootstrapResult<TBody>> {
   143→  const method = methodFor(options);
   144→  const format = errorFormat(options);
   145→  const corsPreflight = options.corsPreflight ?? true;
   146→
   147→  if (corsPreflight && req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
   148→    return {
   149→      ok: false,
   150→      response: jsonResponse({ ok: true }),
   151→    };
   152→  }
   153→
   154→  const runtimeResult = initEdgeRuntime(
   155→    options.functionName,
   156→    options.logPrefix,
   157→    options.runtimeOptions,
   158→  );
   159→  if (!runtimeResult.ok) {
   160→    return {
   161→      ok: false,
   162→      response: operationFailureResponse(runtimeResult, 500),
   163→    };
   164→  }
   165→
   166→  const { supabaseAdmin, logger } = runtimeResult.value;
   167→
   168→  if (req.method !== method) {
   169→    logger.warn('Method not allowed', { method: req.method });
   170→    await logger.flush();
   171→    return {
   172→      ok: false,
   173→      response: buildErrorResponse('Method not allowed', 405, format, { Allow: method }),
   174→    };
   175→  }
   176→
   177→  let body: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   178→
   179→  if (parseMode(options) === 'strict') {
   180→    const parsedBody = await parseJsonBodyStrict(req, logger, {
   181→      message: options.invalidJsonMessage,
   182→      nonObjectMessage: options.invalidJsonShapeMessage,
   183→    });
   184→    if (!parsedBody.ok) {
   185→      return {
   186→        ok: false,
   187→        response: format === 'text'
   188→          ? buildErrorResponse(parsedBody.message, 400, 'text')
   189→          : parseJsonFailureResponse(parsedBody),
   190→      };
   191→    }
   192→    body = parsedBody.value;
   193→  } else if (parseMode(options) === 'loose') {
   194→    const parsedBody = await parseJsonBody(req, logger, {
   195→      mode: 'loose',
   196→      message: options.invalidJsonMessage,
   197→      nonObjectMessage: options.invalidJsonShapeMessage,
   198→    });
   199→    if (!parsedBody.ok) {
   200→      return {
   201→        ok: false,
   202→        response: format === 'text'
   203→          ? buildErrorResponse(parsedBody.message, 400, 'text')
   204→          : parseJsonFailureResponse(parsedBody),
   205→      };
   206→    }
   207→    body = parsedBody.value;
   208→  }
   209→
   210→  let auth: AuthResult | null = null;
   211→  if (authIsRequired(options)) {
   212→    auth = await authenticateRequest(
   213→      req,
   214→      supabaseAdmin,
   215→      options.logPrefix,
   216→      options.auth?.options ?? {},
   217→    );
   218→    if (!auth.success) {
   219→      return {
   220→        ok: false,
   221→        response: buildErrorResponse(
   222→          auth.error || 'Authentication failed',
   223→          auth.statusCode || 401,
   224→          format,
   225→        ),
   226→      };
   227→    }
   228→
   229→    if (options.auth?.requireServiceRole && !auth.isServiceRole) {
   230→      return {
   231→        ok: false,
   232→        response: buildErrorResponse('Unauthorized - service role required', 403, format),
   233→      };
   234→    }
   235→  }
   236→
   237→  return {
   238→    ok: true,
   239→    value: {
   240→      req,
   241→      body: body as TBody,
   242→      supabaseAdmin,
   243→      logger,
   244→      auth,
   245→    },
   246→  };
   247→}
   248→
   249→function toErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
   250→  if (error instanceof Error) {
   251→    return error.message;
   252→  }
   253→  return String(error);
   254→}
   255→
   256→/**
   257→ * High-level request wrapper that standardizes edge endpoint choreography:
   258→ * bootstrap -> handler -> unified unexpected-error response -> guaranteed flush.
   259→ */
   260→export async function withEdgeRequest<TBody extends Record<string, unknown> = Record<string, unknown>>(
   261→  req: Request,
   262→  options: EdgeBootstrapOptions,
   263→  handler: (context: EdgeRequestContext<TBody>) => Promise<Response>,
   264→): Promise<Response> {
   265→  const bootstrap = await bootstrapEdgeHandler<TBody>(req, options);
   266→  if (!bootstrap.ok) {
   267→    return bootstrap.response;
   268→  }
   269→
   270→  const context = bootstrap.value;
   271→  try {
   272→    return await handler(context);
   273→  } catch (error: unknown) {
   274→    context.logger.critical('Unexpected error', {
   275→      error: toErrorMessage(error),
   276→    });
   277→    return buildErrorResponse('Internal server error', 500, errorFormat(options));
   278→  } finally {
   279→    await context.logger.flush();
   280→  }
   281→}
   282→

import React from 'react';
import { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
import { FinalVideoSection } from '../FinalVideoSection';
import { ShotSettingsProvider, ShotSettingsContextValue } from './ShotSettingsContext';
import { HeaderSection, TimelineSection, ModalsSection, GenerationSection } from './sections';
import { LoraModel } from '@/shared/components/LoraSelectorModal';

export interface ShotEditorLayoutProps {
  contextValue: ShotSettingsContextValue;

  header: {
    onBack: () => void;
    onPreviousShot?: () => void;
    onNextShot?: () => void;
    hasPrevious?: boolean;
    hasNext?: boolean;
    onUpdateShotName?: (name: string) => void;
    onNameClick: () => void;
    onNameSave: () => void;
    onNameCancel: (e?: React.MouseEvent) => void;
    onNameKeyDown: (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => void;
    headerContainerRef?: (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => void;
    centerSectionRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
    isSticky?: boolean;
  };

  finalVideo: {
    selectedShotId: string;
    projectId: string;
    effectiveAspectRatio?: string;
    onApplySettingsFromTask?: (taskId: string, replaceImages: boolean, inputImages: string[]) => void;
    onJoinSegmentsClick: () => void;
    selectedOutputId: string | null;
    onSelectedOutputChange: (id: string | null) => void;
    parentGenerations: GenerationRow[];
    initialParentGenerations: GenerationRow[];
    segmentProgress?: { completed: number; total: number };
    isSegmentOutputsLoading: boolean;
    getFinalVideoCount?: (shotId: string | null) => number | null;
    onDeleteFinalVideo: (generationId: string) => void;
    isClearingFinalVideo: boolean;
    videoGalleryRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
    generateVideosCardRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
  };

  timeline: {
    timelineSectionRef?: (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => void;
    isModeReady: boolean;
    settingsError: string | null;
    isPhone: boolean;
    generationMode?: 'batch' | 'timeline' | 'by-pair';
    onGenerationModeChange?: (mode: 'batch' | 'timeline' | 'by-pair') => void;
    batchVideoFrames: number;
    onBatchVideoFramesChange: (frames: number) => void;
    aspectAdjustedColumns: 2 | 3 | 4 | 6;
    pendingFramePositions: Map<string, number>;
    onPendingPositionApplied: (generationId: string) => void;
    onSelectionChange: (hasSelection: boolean) => void;
    prompt: string;
    onPromptChange: (prompt: string) => void;
    negativePrompt: string;
    onNegativePromptChange: (prompt: string) => void;
    smoothContinuations?: boolean;
    onDragStateChange?: (isDragging: boolean) => void;
    getHasStructureVideo?: (shotId: string | null) => boolean | null;
  };

  generation: {
    ctaContainerRef?: (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => void;
    swapButtonRef: React.RefObject<HTMLButtonElement>;
    joinSegmentsSectionRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
    parentVariantName?: string;
    parentOnVariantNameChange?: (name: string) => void;
    parentIsGeneratingVideo?: boolean;
    parentVideoJustQueued?: boolean;
  };

  modals: {
    isLoraModalOpen: boolean;
    onLoraModalClose: () => void;
    onAddLora: (lora: LoraModel, isManualAction?: boolean, initialStrength?: number) => void;
    onRemoveLora: (loraId: string) => void;
    onUpdateLoraStrength: (loraId: string, strength: number) => void;
    selectedLoras: Array<{ id: string; name: string; strength: number; path?: string }>;
    isSettingsModalOpen: boolean;
    onSettingsModalOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  };
}

export const ShotEditorLayout: React.FC<ShotEditorLayoutProps> = ({
  contextValue,
  header,
  finalVideo,
  timeline,
  generation,
  modals,
}) => {
  return (
    <ShotSettingsProvider value={contextValue}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-y-4 pb-4">
        <HeaderSection
          callbacks={{
            onBack: header.onBack,
            onPreviousShot: header.onPreviousShot,
            onNextShot: header.onNextShot,
            hasPrevious: header.hasPrevious,
            hasNext: header.hasNext,
            onUpdateShotName: header.onUpdateShotName,
            onNameClick: header.onNameClick,
            onNameSave: header.onNameSave,
            onNameCancel: header.onNameCancel,
            onNameKeyDown: header.onNameKeyDown,
          }}
          layout={{
            headerContainerRef: header.headerContainerRef,
            centerSectionRef: header.centerSectionRef,
            isSticky: header.isSticky,
          }}
        />

        <div ref={finalVideo.videoGalleryRef} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <FinalVideoSection
            shotId={finalVideo.selectedShotId}
            projectId={finalVideo.projectId}
            projectAspectRatio={finalVideo.effectiveAspectRatio}
            onApplySettingsFromTask={finalVideo.onApplySettingsFromTask}
            onJoinSegmentsClick={finalVideo.onJoinSegmentsClick}
            selectedParentId={finalVideo.selectedOutputId}
            onSelectedParentChange={finalVideo.onSelectedOutputChange}
            parentGenerations={finalVideo.parentGenerations.length > 0 ? finalVideo.parentGenerations : finalVideo.initialParentGenerations}
            segmentProgress={finalVideo.segmentProgress}
            isParentLoading={finalVideo.isSegmentOutputsLoading && finalVideo.initialParentGenerations.length === 0}
            getFinalVideoCount={finalVideo.getFinalVideoCount}
            onDelete={finalVideo.onDeleteFinalVideo}
            isDeleting={finalVideo.isClearingFinalVideo}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <TimelineSection
            timelineSectionRef={timeline.timelineSectionRef}
            isModeReady={timeline.isModeReady}
            settingsError={timeline.settingsError}
            isMobile={timeline.isPhone}
            generationMode={timeline.generationMode}
            onGenerationModeChange={timeline.onGenerationModeChange}
            batchVideoFrames={timeline.batchVideoFrames}
            onBatchVideoFramesChange={timeline.onBatchVideoFramesChange}
            columns={timeline.aspectAdjustedColumns}
            pendingPositions={timeline.pendingFramePositions}
            onPendingPositionApplied={timeline.onPendingPositionApplied}
            onSelectionChange={timeline.onSelectionChange}
            defaultPrompt={timeline.prompt}
            onDefaultPromptChange={timeline.onPromptChange}
            defaultNegativePrompt={timeline.negativePrompt}
            onDefaultNegativePromptChange={timeline.onNegativePromptChange}
            maxFrameLimit={81}
            smoothContinuations={timeline.smoothContinuations}
            selectedOutputId={finalVideo.selectedOutputId}
            onSelectedOutputChange={finalVideo.onSelectedOutputChange}
            onDragStateChange={timeline.onDragStateChange}
            cachedHasStructureVideo={timeline.getHasStructureVideo?.(finalVideo.selectedShotId) ?? false}
          />

          <GenerationSection
            refs={{
              generateVideosCardRef: finalVideo.generateVideosCardRef,
              ctaContainerRef: generation.ctaContainerRef,
              swapButtonRef: generation.swapButtonRef,
              joinSegmentsSectionRef: generation.joinSegmentsSectionRef,
            }}
            cta={{
              parentVariantName: generation.parentVariantName,
              parentOnVariantNameChange: generation.parentOnVariantNameChange,
              parentIsGeneratingVideo: generation.parentIsGeneratingVideo,
              parentVideoJustQueued: generation.parentVideoJustQueued,
            }}
          />
        </div>

        <ModalsSection
          isLoraModalOpen={modals.isLoraModalOpen}
          onLoraModalClose={modals.onLoraModalClose}
          onAddLora={modals.onAddLora}
          onRemoveLora={modals.onRemoveLora}
          onUpdateLoraStrength={modals.onUpdateLoraStrength}
          selectedLoras={modals.selectedLoras}
          isSettingsModalOpen={modals.isSettingsModalOpen}
          onSettingsModalOpenChange={modals.onSettingsModalOpenChange}
        />
      </div>
    </ShotSettingsProvider>
  );
};
```

    15→export function useAdvancedSettingsHandlers({

    17→  settings,
    18→  shotDefaults,
    19→  effectiveLoras,
    20→  openLoraModal,
    21→}: UseAdvancedSettingsHandlersParams) {
    22→  const handleMotionModeChange = useCallback(

    25→        motionMode: mode,
    26→        phaseConfig:
    27→          mode === 'basic' && !settings.selectedPhasePresetId
    28→            ? undefined
    29→            : (settings.phaseConfig ?? shotDefaults?.phaseConfig),
    30→      });
    31→    },
    32→    [onChange, settings.phaseConfig, settings.selectedPhasePresetId, shotDefaults?.phaseConfig]

    39→      });
    40→    },
    41→    [onChange]

    43→

    49→      });
    50→    },

    52→  );
    53→
    54→  const handlePhasePresetRemove = useCallback(() => {

    67→

import React from 'react';
import { ShotEditorProps } from './state/types';
import { ShotEditorLayout } from './ShotEditorLayout';
import { useShotEditorController } from './useShotEditorController';

const ShotSettingsEditor: React.FC<ShotEditorProps> = React.memo((props) => {
  const { hasSelectedShot, layoutProps } = useShotEditorController(props);

  if (!hasSelectedShot) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-64">
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Shot not found</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return <ShotEditorLayout {...layoutProps} />;
});

export { ShotSettingsEditor };
```

1→import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2→import {
     3→  BUILTIN_I2V_PRESET,
     4→  BUILTIN_VACE_PRESET,
     5→  SEGMENT_I2V_FEATURED_PRESET_IDS,
     6→  SEGMENT_VACE_FEATURED_PRESET_IDS,
     7→  detectGenerationMode,
     8→} from '../segmentSettingsUtils';
     9→import type { SegmentSettings, SegmentSettingsFormProps } from '../types';
    10→
    11→interface UseAdvancedSettingsStateParams {
    12→  modelName?: string;
    13→  settings: SegmentSettings;
    14→  shotDefaults?: SegmentSettingsFormProps['shotDefaults'];
    15→}
    16→
    17→export function useAdvancedSettingsState({
    18→  modelName,
    19→  settings,
    20→  shotDefaults,
    21→}: UseAdvancedSettingsStateParams) {
    22→  const [showAdvanced, setShowAdvanced] = useState(false);
    23→  const [isLoraModalOpen, setIsLoraModalOpen] = useState(false);
    24→
    25→  const generationMode = useMemo(
    26→    () => detectGenerationMode(modelName),
    27→    [modelName]
    28→  );
    29→  const builtinPreset = useMemo(
    30→    () => (generationMode === 'vace' ? BUILTIN_VACE_PRESET : BUILTIN_I2V_PRESET),
    31→    [generationMode]
    32→  );
    33→  const featuredPresetIds = useMemo(
    34→    () =>
    35→      generationMode === 'vace'
    36→        ? SEGMENT_VACE_FEATURED_PRESET_IDS
    37→        : SEGMENT_I2V_FEATURED_PRESET_IDS,
    38→    [generationMode]
    39→  );
    40→  const effectiveLoras = useMemo(
    41→    () =>
    42→      settings.loras !== undefined ? settings.loras : (shotDefaults?.loras ?? []),
    43→    [settings.loras, shotDefaults?.loras]
    44→  );
    45→
    46→  const isUsingMotionModeDefault =
    47→    settings.motionMode === undefined && !!shotDefaults?.motionMode;
    48→  const isUsingPhaseConfigDefault =
    49→    settings.phaseConfig === undefined && !!shotDefaults?.phaseConfig;
    50→  const isUsingLorasDefault =
    51→    settings.loras === undefined && (shotDefaults?.loras?.length ?? 0) > 0;
    52→
    53→  return {
    54→    showAdvanced,
    55→    setShowAdvanced,
    56→    isLoraModalOpen,
    57→    openLoraModal: () => setIsLoraModalOpen(true),
    58→    closeLoraModal: () => setIsLoraModalOpen(false),
    59→    generationMode,
    60→    builtinPreset,
    61→    featuredPresetIds,
    62→    effectiveLoras,
    63→    isUsingMotionDefaults: isUsingMotionModeDefault && isUsingPhaseConfigDefault,
    64→    isUsingLorasDefault,
    65→  };
    66→}
    67→

    21→  const handleCopyParams = useCallback(async () => {
    22→    if (!taskParams) return;
    23→    try {
    24→      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(taskParams, null, 2));
    25→      setParamsCopied(true);
    26→      setTimeout(() => setParamsCopied(false), 2000);
    27→    } catch (err) {
    28→      normalizeAndPresentError(err, { context: 'TaskDetailsModal', showToast: false });
    29→    }
    30→  }, [taskParams]);
    31→
    32→  const handleCopyId = useCallback(() => {
    33→    if (!taskId) return;
    34→    navigator.clipboard.writeText(taskId);
    35→    setIdCopied(true);
    36→    setTimeout(() => setIdCopied(false), 2000);
    37→  }, [taskId]);
    38→
    39→  return {
    40→    replaceImages,
    41→    setReplaceImages,
    42→    showDetailedParams,
    43→    setShowDetailedParams,
    44→    showAllImages,
    45→    setShowAllImages,
    46→    showFullPrompt,
    47→    setShowFullPrompt,
    48→    showFullNegativePrompt,
    49→    setShowFullNegativePrompt,
    50→    paramsCopied,
    51→    idCopied,
    52→    handleCopyParams,
    53→    handleCopyId,
    54→  };
    55→}
    56→
```

1→/**
     2→ * Request parsing and validation for complete_task
     3→ */
     4→
     5→import type { SupabaseClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2.39.7';
     6→import {
     7→  edgeErrorResponse,
     8→  parseJsonBodyStrict,
     9→  parseJsonFailureResponse,
    10→} from '../_shared/edgeRequest.ts';
    11→import { getContentType } from './params.ts';
    12→
    13→// ===== TYPES =====
    14→
    15→/**
    16→ * Upload mode for complete-task requests
    17→ */
    18→type UploadMode = 'base64' | 'presigned' | 'reference';
    19→
    20→/**
    21→ * Parsed request data from complete-task endpoint
    22→ */
    23→interface ParsedRequest {
    24→  taskId: string;
    25→  mode: UploadMode;
    26→  filename: string;
    27→  // MODE 1 (base64) specific
    28→  fileData?: Uint8Array;
    29→  fileContentType?: string;
    30→  thumbnailData?: Uint8Array;
    31→  thumbnailFilename?: string;
    32→  thumbnailContentType?: string;
    33→  // MODE 3/4 (storage path) specific
    34→  storagePath?: string;
    35→  thumbnailStoragePath?: string;
    36→  // For MODE 3 validation (set during parsing)
    37→  storagePathTaskId?: string; // The task_id extracted from storage_path
    38→  requiresOrchestratorCheck?: boolean; // True if path task_id != request task_id
    39→}
    40→
    41→/**
    42→ * Result of request parsing - either success with data or error with response
    43→ */
    44→type ParseResult =
    45→  | { success: true; data: ParsedRequest }
    46→  | { success: false; response: Response };
    47→
    48→interface CompleteTaskRequestBody {
    49→  task_id?: unknown;
    50→  file_data?: unknown;
    51→  filename?: unknown;
    52→  first_frame_data?: unknown;
    53→  first_frame_filename?: unknown;
    54→  storage_path?: unknown;
    55→  thumbnail_storage_path?: unknown;
    56→}
    57→
    58→// ===== REQUEST PARSING =====
    59→
    60→const badRequest = (message: string, errorCode = 'invalid_request'): ParseResult => ({
    61→  success: false,
    62→  response: edgeErrorResponse(
    63→    {
    64→      errorCode,
    65→      message,
    66→      recoverable: false,
    67→    },
    68→    400,
    69→  ),
    70→});
    71→
    72→const asString = (value: unknown): string | undefined =>
    73→  typeof value === "string" && value.length > 0 ? value : undefined;
    74→
    75→function parseStoragePathRequest(body: CompleteTaskRequestBody): ParseResult {
    76→  const taskId = asString(body.task_id);
    77→  const storagePath = asString(body.storage_path);
    78→  const thumbnailStoragePath = asString(body.thumbnail_storage_path);
    79→
    80→  if (!taskId) {
    81→    return badRequest("task_id required", 'task_id_required');
    82→  }
    83→  if (!storagePath) {
    84→    return badRequest("storage_path required", 'storage_path_required');
    85→  }
    86→
    87→  const pathParts = storagePath.split('/');
    88→  const isMode3Format = pathParts.length >= 4 && pathParts[1] === 'tasks';
    89→  const mode: UploadMode = isMode3Format ? 'presigned' : 'reference';
    90→
    91→  if (mode === 'reference' && pathParts.length < 2) {
    92→    return badRequest(
    93→      "Invalid storage_path format. Must be at least userId/filename",
    94→      'invalid_storage_path_format',
    95→    );
    96→  }
    97→
    98→  let requiresOrchestratorCheck = false;
    99→  let storagePathTaskId: string | undefined;
   100→
   101→  if (mode === 'presigned') {
   102→    storagePathTaskId = pathParts[2];
   103→    if (storagePathTaskId !== taskId) {
   104→      requiresOrchestratorCheck = true;
   105→    }
   106→
   107→    if (thumbnailStoragePath) {
   108→      const thumbParts = thumbnailStoragePath.split('/');
   109→      if (thumbParts.length < 4 || thumbParts[1] !== 'tasks') {
   110→        return badRequest("Invalid thumbnail_storage_path format.", 'invalid_thumbnail_storage_path_format');
   111→      }
   112→      if (thumbParts[2] !== taskId) {
   113→        requiresOrchestratorCheck = true;
   114→      }
   115→    }
   116→  }
   117→
   118→  return {
   119→    success: true,
   120→    data: {
   121→      taskId,
   122→      mode,
   123→      filename: pathParts[pathParts.length - 1],
   124→      storagePath,
   125→      thumbnailStoragePath,
   126→      storagePathTaskId,
   127→      requiresOrchestratorCheck,
   128→    },
   129→  };
   130→}
   131→
   132→function parseBase64Request(body: CompleteTaskRequestBody): ParseResult {
   133→  const taskId = asString(body.task_id);
   134→  const fileData = asString(body.file_data);
   135→  const filename = asString(body.filename);
   136→  const firstFrameData = asString(body.first_frame_data);
   137→  const firstFrameFilename = asString(body.first_frame_filename);
   138→
   139→  if (!taskId || !fileData || !filename) {
   140→    return badRequest(
   141→      "task_id, file_data (base64), and filename required (or use storage_path for pre-uploaded files)",
   142→      'missing_base64_upload_fields',
   143→    );
   144→  }
   145→
   146→  if (firstFrameData && !firstFrameFilename) {
   147→    return badRequest("first_frame_filename required when first_frame_data is provided", 'first_frame_filename_required');
   148→  }
   149→  if (firstFrameFilename && !firstFrameData) {
   150→    return badRequest("first_frame_data required when first_frame_filename is provided", 'first_frame_data_required');
   151→  }
   152→
   153→  let fileBuffer: Uint8Array;
   154→  try {
   155→    fileBuffer = Uint8Array.from(atob(fileData), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
   156→  } catch (error) {
   157→    console.error("[RequestParser] Base64 decode error:", error);
   158→    return badRequest("Invalid base64 file_data", 'invalid_base64_file_data');
   159→  }
   160→
   161→  let thumbnailBuffer: Uint8Array | undefined;
   162→  let thumbnailFilename: string | undefined;
   163→  if (firstFrameData && firstFrameFilename) {
   164→    try {
   165→      thumbnailBuffer = Uint8Array.from(atob(firstFrameData), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
   166→      thumbnailFilename = firstFrameFilename;
   167→    } catch (error) {
   168→      console.error("[RequestParser] Thumbnail base64 decode error:", error);
   169→      // Continue without thumbnail - non-fatal
   170→    }
   171→  }
   172→
   173→  return {
   174→    success: true,
   175→    data: {
   176→      taskId,
   177→      mode: 'base64',
   178→      filename,
   179→      fileData: fileBuffer,
   180→      fileContentType: getContentType(filename),
   181→      thumbnailData: thumbnailBuffer,
   182→      thumbnailFilename,
   183→      thumbnailContentType: thumbnailFilename ? getContentType(thumbnailFilename) : undefined,
   184→    },
   185→  };
   186→}
   187→
   188→/**
   189→ * Parse and validate the incoming request
   190→ * Does structural validation only - security checks (orchestrator validation) happen later
   191→ */
   192→export async function parseCompleteTaskRequest(req: Request): Promise<ParseResult> {
   193→  const contentType = req.headers.get("content-type") || "";
   194→
   195→  // Multipart not supported
   196→  if (contentType.includes("multipart/form-data")) {
   197→    return badRequest(
   198→      "Multipart upload (MODE 2) is not supported. Use MODE 1 (base64 JSON) or MODE 3 (pre-signed URL).",
   199→      'multipart_not_supported',
   200→    );
   201→  }
   202→
   203→  const parsedBody = await parseJsonBodyStrict(req, undefined, {
   204→    message: 'Invalid JSON body',
   205→    nonObjectMessage: 'JSON body must be an object',
   206→  });
   207→  if (!parsedBody.ok) {
   208→    return {
   209→      success: false,
   210→      response: parseJsonFailureResponse(parsedBody, 400),
   211→    };
   212→  }
   213→  const body = parsedBody.value as CompleteTaskRequestBody;
   214→
   215→  if (asString(body.storage_path)) {
   216→    return parseStoragePathRequest(body);
   217→  }
   218→
   219→  return parseBase64Request(body);
   220→}
   221→
   222→// ===== SECURITY VALIDATION =====
   223→
   224→/**
   225→ * Validate that a storage path is allowed for the given task
   226→ * Called after Supabase client is available for orchestrator check
   227→ */
   228→export async function validateStoragePathSecurity(
   229→  supabase: SupabaseClient,
   230→  taskId: string,
   231→  storagePath: string,
   232→  storagePathTaskId: string | undefined
   233→): Promise<{ allowed: boolean; error?: string }> {
   234→  // If path task_id matches request task_id, it's allowed
   235→  if (!storagePathTaskId || storagePathTaskId === taskId) {
   236→    return { allowed: true };
   237→  }
   238→
   239→  // Check if this is an orchestrator task (allowed to reference other task outputs)
   240→  const { data: task, error } = await supabase
   241→    .from('tasks')
   242→    .select('task_type')
   243→    .eq('id', taskId)
   244→    .single();
   245→
   246→  if (error) {
   247→    console.error(`[SecurityCheck] Error fetching task for validation: ${error.message}`);
   248→    return { allowed: false, error: "storage_path does not match task_id. Files must be uploaded for the correct task." };
   249→  }
   250→
   251→  const isOrchestrator = task?.task_type?.includes('orchestrator');
   252→  if (isOrchestrator) {
   253→    return { allowed: true };
   254→  }
   255→
   256→  console.error(`[SecurityCheck] ❌ Non-orchestrator task attempting to reference different task's output`);
   257→  return { allowed: false, error: "storage_path does not match task_id. Files must be uploaded for the correct task." };

1→import type { PortionSelection } from '@/shared/components/VideoPortionTimeline';
     2→
     3→export interface ReplaceFrameRangeSelection {
     4→  start_frame: number;
     5→  end_frame: number;
     6→  start_time: number;
     7→  end_time: number;
     8→  frame_count: number;
     9→  gap_frame_count: number;
    10→  prompt: string;
    11→}
    12→
    13→export function selectionsToFrameRanges(
    14→  selections: PortionSelection[],
    15→  fps: number,
    16→  totalDuration: number,
    17→  globalGapFrameCount: number,
    18→  globalPrompt: string
    19→): ReplaceFrameRangeSelection[] {
    20→  const totalFrames = Math.round(totalDuration * fps);
    21→
    22→  return selections.map(selection => {
    23→    const startFrame = Math.max(0, Math.round(selection.start * fps));
    24→    const endFrame = Math.min(totalFrames, Math.round(selection.end * fps));
    25→    return {
    26→      start_frame: startFrame,
    27→      end_frame: endFrame,
    28→      start_time: selection.start,
    29→      end_time: selection.end,
    30→      frame_count: endFrame - startFrame,
    31→      gap_frame_count: selection.gapFrameCount ?? globalGapFrameCount,
    32→      prompt: selection.prompt || globalPrompt,
    33→    };
    34→  });
    35→}
    36→
    37→function getMinKeeperFrames(totalFrames: number, frameRanges: ReplaceFrameRangeSelection[]): number {
    38→  const sortedPortions = [...frameRanges].sort((a, b) => a.start_frame - b.start_frame);
    39→  let minKeeperFrames = totalFrames;
    40→
    41→  if (sortedPortions.length > 0) {
    42→    const firstKeeperLength = sortedPortions[0].start_frame;
    43→    if (firstKeeperLength > 0) {
    44→      minKeeperFrames = Math.min(minKeeperFrames, firstKeeperLength);
    45→    }
    46→  }
    47→
    48→  for (let i = 0; i < sortedPortions.length - 1; i++) {
    49→    const keeperLength = sortedPortions[i + 1].start_frame - sortedPortions[i].end_frame;
    50→    if (keeperLength > 0) {
    51→      minKeeperFrames = Math.min(minKeeperFrames, keeperLength);
    52→    }
    53→  }
    54→
    55→  if (sortedPortions.length > 0) {
    56→    const lastKeeperLength = totalFrames - sortedPortions[sortedPortions.length - 1].end_frame;
    57→    if (lastKeeperLength > 0) {
    58→      minKeeperFrames = Math.min(minKeeperFrames, lastKeeperLength);
    59→    }
    60→  }
    61→
    62→  return minKeeperFrames;
    63→}
    64→
    65→export function calculateMaxContextFrames({
    66→  videoFps,
    67→  videoDuration,
    68→  frameRanges,
    69→}: {
    70→  videoFps: number | null;
    71→  videoDuration: number;
    72→  frameRanges: ReplaceFrameRangeSelection[];
    73→}): number {
    74→  if (!videoFps || !videoDuration || frameRanges.length === 0) {
    75→    return 30;
    76→  }
    77→
    78→  const totalFrames = Math.round(videoDuration * videoFps);
    79→  const minKeeperFrames = getMinKeeperFrames(totalFrames, frameRanges);
    80→  return Math.max(4, minKeeperFrames - 1);
    81→}
    82→
    83→export function capContextFrameCountForRanges({
    84→  contextFrameCount,
    85→  totalFrames,
    86→  frameRanges,
    87→}: {
    88→  contextFrameCount: number;
    89→  totalFrames: number;
    90→  frameRanges: ReplaceFrameRangeSelection[];
    91→}): number {
    92→  const minKeeperFrames = getMinKeeperFrames(totalFrames, frameRanges);
    93→  const safeMaxContextFrames = Math.max(1, minKeeperFrames - 1);
    94→  return Math.min(contextFrameCount, safeMaxContextFrames);
    95→}
    96→

1→function quantizeGapFramesFromDuration(duration: number, fps: number): number {
     2→  const frameCount = Math.round(duration * fps);
     3→  return quantizeGapFrameCount(frameCount);
     4→}
     5→
     6→function getMaxGapFramesForContext(contextFrameCount: number): number {
     7→  return Math.max(1, 81 - (contextFrameCount * 2));
     8→}
     9→
    10→export function quantizeGapFrameCount(frameCount: number): number {
    11→  const n = Math.round((frameCount - 1) / 4);
    12→  return Math.max(1, n * 4 + 1);
    13→}
    14→
    15→export function calculateGapFramesFromRange({
    16→  start,
    17→  end,
    18→  fps,
    19→  fallbackGapFrameCount,
    20→  contextFrameCount,
    21→}: {
    22→  start: number;
    23→  end: number;
    24→  fps: number | null;
    25→  fallbackGapFrameCount: number;
    26→  contextFrameCount: number;
    27→}): number {
    28→  if (!fps || end <= start) {
    29→    return fallbackGapFrameCount;
    30→  }
    31→
    32→  const quantized = quantizeGapFramesFromDuration(end - start, fps);
    33→  return Math.min(quantized, getMaxGapFramesForContext(contextFrameCount));
    34→}

1→import type { PortionSelection } from '@/shared/components/VideoPortionTimeline';
     2→
     3→const DEFAULT_SELECTION_START_RATIO = 0.1;
     4→const DEFAULT_SELECTION_END_RATIO = 0.2;
     5→const FALLBACK_SELECTION_START_RATIO = 0.4;
     6→const FALLBACK_SELECTION_END_RATIO = 0.5;
     7→const NEW_SELECTION_WIDTH_RATIO = 0.1;
     8→const NEW_SELECTION_GAP_RATIO = 0.1;
     9→
    10→export function getDefaultSelectionRange(videoDuration: number): { start: number; end: number } {
    11→  return {
    12→    start: videoDuration * DEFAULT_SELECTION_START_RATIO,
    13→    end: videoDuration * DEFAULT_SELECTION_END_RATIO,
    14→  };
    15→}
    16→
    17→export function getNewSelectionRange(selections: PortionSelection[], videoDuration: number): { start: number; end: number } {
    18→  const sortedSelections = [...selections].sort((a, b) => a.end - b.end);
    19→  const lastSelection = sortedSelections[sortedSelections.length - 1];
    20→  if (!lastSelection) {
    21→    return getDefaultSelectionRange(videoDuration);
    22→  }
    23→
    24→  const selectionWidth = videoDuration * NEW_SELECTION_WIDTH_RATIO;
    25→  const gap = videoDuration * NEW_SELECTION_GAP_RATIO;
    26→
    27→  const afterStart = lastSelection.end + gap;
    28→  const afterEnd = afterStart + selectionWidth;
    29→  if (afterEnd <= videoDuration) {
    30→    return { start: afterStart, end: afterEnd };
    31→  }
    32→
    33→  const firstSelection = sortedSelections[0];
    34→  const beforeEnd = firstSelection.start - gap;
    35→  const beforeStart = beforeEnd - selectionWidth;
    36→  if (beforeStart >= 0) {
    37→    return { start: beforeStart, end: beforeEnd };
    38→  }
    39→
    40→  return {
    41→    start: videoDuration * FALLBACK_SELECTION_START_RATIO,
    42→    end: videoDuration * FALLBACK_SELECTION_END_RATIO,
    43→  };
    44→}
    45→

1→import { generateUUID } from '@/shared/lib/taskCreation';
     2→import type { JoinClipsSettings } from '@/shared/lib/joinClipsDefaults';
     3→import type { TransitionPrompt, VideoClip } from '../types';
     4→import { createEmptyClip } from './clipFactory';
     5→
     6→interface InitialClipsResult {
     7→  clips: VideoClip[];
     8→  transitionPrompts: TransitionPrompt[];
     9→  posterUrlsToPreload: string[];
    10→}
    11→
    12→export function buildInitialClipsFromSettings(
    13→  settings: JoinClipsSettings,
    14→): InitialClipsResult {
    15→  const initialClips: VideoClip[] = [];
    16→  const posterUrlsToPreload: string[] = [];
    17→  let transitionPrompts: TransitionPrompt[] = [];
    18→
    19→  // Try loading from new multi-clip format
    20→  if (settings.clips && settings.clips.length > 0) {
    21→    settings.clips.forEach(clip => {
    22→      if (clip.url) {
    23→        initialClips.push({
    24→          id: generateUUID(),
    25→          url: clip.url,
    26→          posterUrl: clip.posterUrl,
    27→          finalFrameUrl: clip.finalFrameUrl,
    28→          durationSeconds: clip.durationSeconds,
    29→          loaded: false,
    30→          playing: false,
    31→        });
    32→        if (clip.posterUrl) posterUrlsToPreload.push(clip.posterUrl);
    33→      }
    34→    });
    35→
    36→    // Load transition prompts
    37→    if (settings.transitionPrompts && settings.transitionPrompts.length > 0) {
    38→      transitionPrompts = settings.transitionPrompts
    39→        .map(tp => ({
    40→          id: initialClips[tp.clipIndex]?.id || '',
    41→          prompt: tp.prompt,
    42→        }))
    43→        .filter(p => p.id);
    44→    }
    45→  }
    46→  // Fallback to legacy two-video format
    47→  else if (settings.startingVideoUrl || settings.endingVideoUrl) {
    48→    if (settings.startingVideoUrl) {
    49→      initialClips.push({
    50→        id: generateUUID(),
    51→        url: settings.startingVideoUrl,
    52→        posterUrl: settings.startingVideoPosterUrl,
    53→        loaded: false,
    54→        playing: false,
    55→      });
    56→      if (settings.startingVideoPosterUrl) {
    57→        posterUrlsToPreload.push(settings.startingVideoPosterUrl);
    58→      }
    59→    }
    60→
    61→    if (settings.endingVideoUrl) {
    62→      initialClips.push({
    63→        id: generateUUID(),
    64→        url: settings.endingVideoUrl,
    65→        posterUrl: settings.endingVideoPosterUrl,
    66→        loaded: false,
    67→        playing: false,
    68→      });
    69→      if (settings.endingVideoPosterUrl) {
    70→        posterUrlsToPreload.push(settings.endingVideoPosterUrl);
    71→      }
    72→    }
    73→
    74→    // Initialize transition prompts from legacy format
    75→    // The `prompt` field existed in an older settings schema and may still
    76→    // be present in persisted data even though it's not in the current type.
    77→    const legacyPrompt = (settings as JoinClipsSettings & { prompt?: string }).prompt;
    78→    if (initialClips.length >= 2 && legacyPrompt) {
    79→      transitionPrompts = [
    80→        {
    81→          id: initialClips[1].id,
    82→          prompt: legacyPrompt,
    83→        },
    84→      ];
    85→    }
    86→  }
    87→
    88→  return { clips: initialClips, transitionPrompts, posterUrlsToPreload };
    89→}
    90→
    91→/**
    92→ * Pad initialClips to have at least 2 slots plus one trailing empty slot.
    93→ * This matches the original hook behavior exactly.
    94→ */
    95→export function padClipsWithEmptySlots(initialClips: VideoClip[]): VideoClip[] {
    96→  if (initialClips.length === 0) {
    97→    return [createEmptyClip(), createEmptyClip()];
    98→  }
    99→
   100→  if (initialClips.length < 2) {
   101→    const clipsToAdd = 2 - initialClips.length;
   102→    const emptyClips = Array.from({ length: clipsToAdd }, () => createEmptyClip());
   103→    return [...initialClips, ...emptyClips];
   104→  }
   105→
   106→  // >= 2 clips: add one trailing empty slot
   107→  return [...initialClips, createEmptyClip()];
   108→}
   109→

1→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
     2→
     3→function getLocalStorageKey(projectId: string): string {

     7→const MAX_CACHED_CLIPS_COUNT = 500;
     8→
     9→export function getCachedClipsCount(projectId: string | null): number {
    10→  if (!projectId) return 0;
    11→  try {
    12→    const cached = localStorage.getItem(getLocalStorageKey(projectId));
    13→    if (!cached) return 0;
    14→
    15→    const parsed = Number.parseInt(cached, 10);
    16→    if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed < 0 || parsed > MAX_CACHED_CLIPS_COUNT) {
    17→      normalizeAndPresentError(new Error('Invalid join-clips cached count'), {
    18→        context: 'JoinClipsCache.read.invalid',
    19→        showToast: false,
    20→        logData: { projectId, cached },
    21→      });
    22→      return 0;
    23→    }
    24→    return parsed;
    25→  } catch (error) {
    26→    normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
    27→      context: 'JoinClipsCache.read.error',
    28→      showToast: false,
    29→      logData: { projectId },
    30→    });
    31→    return 0;
    32→  }
    33→}
    34→
    35→export function setCachedClipsCount(projectId: string | null, count: number): void {
    36→  if (!projectId) return;
    37→  try {
    38→    const normalizedCount = Number.isFinite(count) ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(count)) : 0;
    39→    if (normalizedCount > 0) {
    40→      localStorage.setItem(getLocalStorageKey(projectId), normalizedCount.toString());
    41→    } else {
    42→      localStorage.removeItem(getLocalStorageKey(projectId));
    43→    }
    44→  } catch (error) {
    45→    normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
    46→      context: 'JoinClipsCache.write.error',
    47→      showToast: false,
    48→      logData: { projectId, count },
    49→    });
    50→  }
    51→}
    52→

1→/**
     2→ * DataFreshnessManager - Single source of truth for data freshness and polling decisions
     3→ *
     4→ * This class centralizes all decisions about:
     5→ * - Whether data is fresh (based on realtime events)
     6→ * - What polling intervals React Query should use
     7→ * - Realtime connection health status
     8→ */
     9→
    10→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
    11→import {
    12→  buildQueryAgeDiagnostics,
    13→  getLastEventTime,
    14→  isQueryFresh,
    15→  markQueryKeysFresh,
    16→  serializeQueryKey,
    17→  type QueryKeyLike,
    18→} from '@/shared/realtime/dataFreshness/queryFreshness';
    19→import { registerDataFreshnessDebugGlobal } from '@/shared/realtime/dataFreshness/debugGlobal';
    20→import { DataFreshnessSubscribers } from '@/shared/realtime/dataFreshness/subscribers';
    21→
    22→type RealtimeStatus = 'connected' | 'disconnected' | 'error';
    23→type PollingInterval = number | false; // false = no polling
    24→
    25→interface DataFreshnessState {
    26→  realtimeStatus: RealtimeStatus;
    27→  lastEventTimes: Map<string, number>;
    28→  lastStatusChange: number;
    29→  /** Track consecutive fetch failures per query for circuit breaker */
    30→  fetchFailures: Map<string, { count: number; lastFailure: number }>;
    31→}
    32→
    33→class DataFreshnessManager {
    34→  private state: DataFreshnessState = {
    35→    realtimeStatus: 'disconnected',
    36→    lastEventTimes: new Map(),
    37→    lastStatusChange: Date.now(),
    38→    fetchFailures: new Map()
    39→  };
    40→
    41→  private subscribers = new DataFreshnessSubscribers();
    42→
    43→  // Circuit breaker settings
    44→  private readonly CIRCUIT_BREAKER_THRESHOLD = 3; // failures before backing off
    45→  private readonly CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS = 60_000; // 1 minute to reset failure count
    46→
    47→  // Toast throttling - prevent spamming error toasts
    48→  private lastErrorToastTime = 0;
    49→  private readonly ERROR_TOAST_THROTTLE_MS = 30_000; // Only show error toast every 30 seconds
    50→
    51→  private parseQueryKey(rawKey: string): unknown {
    52→    try {
    53→      return JSON.parse(rawKey);
    54→    } catch {
    55→      return rawKey;
    56→    }
    57→  }
    58→
    59→  /**
    60→   * Report realtime connection status change
    61→   */
    62→  onRealtimeStatusChange(status: RealtimeStatus, _reason?: string) {
    63→    const previousStatus = this.state.realtimeStatus;
    64→    const now = Date.now();
    65→
    66→    // Ignore duplicate status changes to keep lastStatusChange stable.
    67→    // This allows the "recently connected" grace period in getPollingInterval to actually expire.
    68→    if (previousStatus === status) {
    69→      return; // Don't update state or notify subscribers for duplicates
    70→    }
    71→    
    72→    // Actual status transition - update state
    73→    this.state.realtimeStatus = status;
    74→    this.state.lastStatusChange = now;
    75→
    76→    // Notify all subscribers that polling intervals may have changed
    77→    this.notifySubscribers();
    78→  }
    79→
    80→  /**
    81→   * Report that realtime events were received for specific queries
    82→   */
    83→  onRealtimeEvent(_eventType: string, affectedQueries: readonly QueryKeyLike[]) {
    84→    const now = Date.now();
    85→    markQueryKeysFresh(this.state.lastEventTimes, affectedQueries, now);
    86→
    87→    // Notify subscribers that data freshness has changed
    88→    this.notifySubscribers();
    89→  }
    90→
    91→  /**
    92→   * Get the appropriate polling interval for a query
    93→   * Returns false to disable polling when realtime is working well
    94→   */
    95→  getPollingInterval(queryKey: QueryKeyLike): PollingInterval {
    96→    const [REDACTED](queryKey);
    97→    const lastEvent = getLastEventTime(this.state.lastEventTimes, queryKey);
    98→    const now = Date.now();
    99→    const timeSinceStatusChange = now - this.state.lastStatusChange;
   100→
   101→    // If realtime is connected, trust it after it's been stable
   102→    if (this.state.realtimeStatus === 'connected') {
   103→      // If realtime just connected (< 30 seconds), give it time to prove it's stable
   104→      if (timeSinceStatusChange < 30000) {
   105→        return 30000; // 30 seconds - wait for realtime to prove it's stable
   106→      }
   107→
   108→      // If realtime has been stable for >30 seconds, trust it.
   109→      // "No events" just means "nothing changed" — not "realtime is broken".
   110→      
   111→      if (lastEvent) {
   112→        const eventAge = now - lastEvent;
   113→        
   114→        if (eventAge < 60000) { // Events within 1 minute
   115→          return false; // Disable polling — realtime is working
   116→        } else if (eventAge < 3 * 60 * 1000) { // Events within 3 minutes
   117→          return 60000; // 60 seconds - light backup polling
   118→        }
   119→      }
   120→
   121→      // Realtime stable for >30s but no events — this is normal when idle.
   122→      // Use very light polling as a safety net only.
   123→      if (timeSinceStatusChange > 5 * 60 * 1000) {
   124→        // Realtime been stable for >5 minutes - fully trust it
   125→        return false; // Disable polling — realtime is stable, idle is OK
   126→      } else {
   127→        // Realtime stable for 30s-5min - use safety net polling
   128→        return 60000; // 60 seconds - safety net while realtime proves long-term stability
   129→      }
   130→    }
   131→
   132→    // If realtime is disconnected or errored, use GRADUATED backoff polling
   133→    // to prevent thundering herd and connection overload
   134→    const disconnectedDuration = now - this.state.lastStatusChange;
   135→
   136→    // Check circuit breaker - if this query has had many failures, back off more
   137→    const failureInfo = this.state.fetchFailures.get(key);
   138→    const hasRecentFailures = failureInfo &&
   139→      (now - failureInfo.lastFailure) < this.CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS &&
   140→      failureInfo.count >= this.CIRCUIT_BREAKER_THRESHOLD;
   141→
   142→    let pollingInterval: number;
   143→
   144→    if (hasRecentFailures) {
   145→      // Circuit breaker triggered - use much longer interval
   146→      pollingInterval = 60_000; // 60 seconds
   147→    } else if (disconnectedDuration < 30_000) {
   148→      // First 30 seconds: 15s polling (give realtime time to reconnect)
   149→      pollingInterval = 15_000;
   150→    } else if (disconnectedDuration < 2 * 60_000) {
   151→      // 30s - 2 minutes: 30s polling
   152→      pollingInterval = 30_000;
   153→    } else if (disconnectedDuration < 5 * 60_000) {
   154→      // 2-5 minutes: 45s polling
   155→      pollingInterval = 45_000;
   156→    } else {
   157→      // >5 minutes: 60s polling (realtime probably has issues)
   158→      pollingInterval = 60_000;
   159→    }
   160→
   161→    return pollingInterval;
   162→  }
   163→
   164→  /**
   165→   * Report a fetch failure for circuit breaker tracking
   166→   */
   167→  onFetchFailure(queryKey: QueryKeyLike, error: Error) {
   168→    const [REDACTED](queryKey);
   169→    const now = Date.now();
   170→    const existing = this.state.fetchFailures.get(key);
   171→
   172→    // Reset count if last failure was too long ago
   173→    const shouldReset = existing && (now - existing.lastFailure) > this.CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS;
   174→
   175→    const newCount = shouldReset ? 1 : (existing?.count || 0) + 1;
   176→    this.state.fetchFailures.set(key, { count: newCount, lastFailure: now });
   177→
   178→    const isCircuitBreakerTriggered = newCount >= this.CIRCUIT_BREAKER_THRESHOLD;
   179→
   180→    const errorType = this.classifyError(error);
   181→
   182→    // Notify subscribers so polling intervals can adjust
   183→    if (isCircuitBreakerTriggered) {
   184→      this.notifySubscribers();
   185→
   186→      // Show user-facing error toast (throttled to prevent spam)
   187→      if (now - this.lastErrorToastTime > this.ERROR_TOAST_THROTTLE_MS) {
   188→        this.lastErrorToastTime = now;
   189→
   190→        this.reportCircuitBreakerError({
   191→          queryKey: key,
   192→          failureCount: newCount,
   193→          errorType,
   194→          error,
   195→        });
   196→      }
   197→    }
   198→  }
   199→
   200→  private reportCircuitBreakerError(input: {
   201→    queryKey: string;
   202→    failureCount: number;
   203→    errorType: string;
   204→    error: Error;
   205→  }) {
   206→    const userMessage = this.getUserFriendlyErrorMessage(input.errorType, input.failureCount);
   207→    normalizeAndPresentError(new Error('Data may be outdated. Will retry automatically.'), {
   208→      context: 'DataFreshnessManager.onFetchFailure',
   209→      toastTitle: userMessage,
   210→      showToast: true,
   211→      logData: {
   212→        [REDACTED](input.queryKey),
   213→        failureCount: input.failureCount,
   214→        errorType: input.errorType,
   215→        errorMessage: input.error.message,
   216→      },
   217→    });
   218→  }
   219→
   220→  /**
   221→   * Get a user-friendly error message based on error type
   222→   */
   223→  private getUserFriendlyErrorMessage(errorType: string, failureCount: number): string {
   224→    switch (errorType) {
   225→      case 'CONNECTION_CLOSED':
   226→      case 'NETWORK_ERROR':
   227→        return 'Connection issue detected';
   228→      case 'TIMEOUT':
   229→        return 'Server is slow to respond';
   230→      case 'RATE_LIMITED':
   231→        return 'Too many requests - slowing down';
   232→      case 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE':
   233→        return 'Service temporarily unavailable';
   234→      case 'AUTH_ERROR':
   235→        return 'Authentication issue - try refreshing';
   236→      default:
   237→        return `Connection issues (${failureCount} failures)`;
   238→    }
   239→  }
   240→
   241→  /**
   242→   * Report a successful fetch (resets circuit breaker)
   243→   */
   244→  onFetchSuccess(queryKey: QueryKeyLike) {
   245→    const [REDACTED](queryKey);
   246→    const existing = this.state.fetchFailures.get(key);
   247→
   248→    if (existing && existing.count > 0) {
   249→      // Reset failure count on success
   250→      this.state.fetchFailures.delete(key);
   251→    }
   252→  }
   253→
   254→  /**
   255→   * Classify error type for better logging and handling
   256→   */
   257→  private classifyError(error: Error): string {
   258→    const message = error.message?.toLowerCase() || '';
   259→
   260→    if (message.includes('connection_closed') || message.includes('err_connection_closed')) {
   261→      return 'CONNECTION_CLOSED';
   262→    }
   263→    if (message.includes('failed to fetch') || message.includes('network')) {
   264→      return 'NETWORK_ERROR';
   265→    }
   266→    if (message.includes('timeout') || message.includes('timed out')) {
   267→      return 'TIMEOUT';
   268→    }
   269→    if (message.includes('abort')) {
   270→      return 'ABORTED';
   271→    }
   272→    if (message.includes('401') || message.includes('unauthorized')) {
   273→      return 'AUTH_ERROR';
   274→    }
   275→    if (message.includes('429') || message.includes('rate limit')) {
   276→      return 'RATE_LIMITED';
   277→    }
   278→    if (message.includes('503') || message.includes('service unavailable')) {
   279→      return 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE';
   280→    }
   281→    return 'UNKNOWN';
   282→  }
   283→
   284→  /**
   285→   * Check if data for a query is considered fresh
   286→   */
   287→  isDataFresh(queryKey: QueryKeyLike, freshnessThreshold: number = 30000): boolean {
   288→    return isQueryFresh(this.state.lastEventTimes, queryKey, freshnessThreshold, Date.now());
   289→  }
   290→
   291→  /**
   292→   * Get current freshness state for debugging
   293→   */
   294→  getDiagnostics() {
   295→    const now = Date.now();
   296→    return {
   297→      realtimeStatus: this.state.realtimeStatus,
   298→      timeSinceStatusChange: now - this.state.lastStatusChange,
   299→      trackedQueries: this.state.lastEventTimes.size,
   300→      queryAges: buildQueryAgeDiagnostics(this.state.lastEventTimes, now, this.parseQueryKey),
   301→      subscriberCount: this.subscribers.size(),
   302→      timestamp: now
   303→    };
   304→  }
   305→
   306→  /**
   307→   * Subscribe to freshness changes
   308→   * Returns unsubscribe function
   309→   */
   310→  subscribe(callback: () => void): () => void {
   311→    return this.subscribers.subscribe(callback);
   312→  }
   313→
   314→  /**
   315→   * Clear all event history (useful for testing or project changes)
   316→   */
   317→  reset() {
   318→    this.state.lastEventTimes.clear();
   319→    this.state.fetchFailures.clear();
   320→    this.state.realtimeStatus = 'disconnected';
   321→    this.state.lastStatusChange = Date.now();
   322→    this.notifySubscribers();
   323→  }
   324→
   325→  /**
   326→   * Manually mark queries as fresh (useful for mutations)
   327→   */
   328→  markQueriesFresh(queryKeys: readonly QueryKeyLike[]) {
   329→    const now = Date.now();
   330→    markQueryKeysFresh(this.state.lastEventTimes, queryKeys, now);
   331→
   332→    this.notifySubscribers();
   333→  }
   334→
   335→  private notifySubscribers() {
   336→    this.subscribers.notify();
   337→  }
   338→}
   339→
   340→// Singleton instance - single source of truth for the entire app
   341→export const dataFreshnessManager = new DataFreshnessManager();
   342→
   343→/** Dev-only helper for wiring debug globals during app bootstrap. */
   344→export function registerDataFreshnessManagerDebugGlobal(): void {
   345→  registerDataFreshnessDebugGlobal(dataFreshnessManager);
   346→}
   347→
```

1→type QueryKeyLike = readonly unknown[];
     2→
     3→export function serializeQueryKey(queryKey: QueryKeyLike): string {
     4→  return JSON.stringify(queryKey);
     5→}
     6→
     7→export function markQueryKeysFresh(
     8→  lastEventTimes: Map<string, number>,
     9→  queryKeys: readonly QueryKeyLike[],
    10→  now: number,
    11→): void {
    12→  queryKeys.forEach((queryKey) => {
    13→    lastEventTimes.set(serializeQueryKey(queryKey), now);
    14→  });
    15→}
    16→
    17→export function getLastEventTime(
    18→  lastEventTimes: Map<string, number>,
    19→  queryKey: QueryKeyLike,
    20→): number | undefined {
    21→  return lastEventTimes.get(serializeQueryKey(queryKey));
    22→}
    23→
    24→export function isQueryFresh(
    25→  lastEventTimes: Map<string, number>,
    26→  queryKey: QueryKeyLike,
    27→  freshnessThreshold: number,
    28→  now: number,
    29→): boolean {
    30→  const lastEvent = getLastEventTime(lastEventTimes, queryKey);
    31→  if (!lastEvent) {
    32→    return false;
    33→  }
    34→  return now - lastEvent < freshnessThreshold;
    35→}
    36→
    37→export function buildQueryAgeDiagnostics(
    38→  lastEventTimes: Map<string, number>,
    39→  now: number,
    40→  parseQueryKey: (rawKey: string) => unknown,
    41→): Array<{ query: unknown; ageMs: number; ageSec: number }> {
    42→  return Array.from(lastEventTimes.entries()).map(([key, time]) => ({
    43→    query: parseQueryKey(key),
    44→    ageMs: now - time,
    45→    ageSec: Math.round((now - time) / 1000),
    46→  }));
    47→}
    48→
    49→export type { QueryKeyLike };
    50→
